Method: 作者提出了一种仅用解码器的any-to-any多模态建模方法Modus，将所有模态对待为输入或输出的对等成员，无需为特定模态设计头部或专用损失函数。Modus支持多模态间的链式生成和交叉验证，并且结构简单，便于扩展。

Result: Modus无需针对模态定制设计，即可‘开箱即用’地在视觉、语言等多个基准任务上达到专业模型或多任务模型的水平。

Conclusion: Modus兼顾了模型通用性与性能，突破了现有any-to-any多模态模型受架构限制带来的瓶颈，有望在科学与通用多模态领域推广应用。

Abstract: Any-to-any models predict any modality from any combination of others within a single network, a formulation used in multimodal vision and vision-language models, and increasingly in scientific domains such as ecology and astronomy. Existing any-to-any models are typically trained from scratch using encoder-decoder or diffusion architectures, impacting their performance and preventing them from using strong pre-trained decoder-only models as a prior. In this work, we investigate decoder-only any-to-any multimodal modeling, which treats all modalities symmetrically and supports arbitrary modalities as inputs and outputs without modality-specific heads, losses, or task pipelines. Because every modality is both an input and an output of the same model, the resulting model, named Modus, can support a range of applications, such as chained generation through intermediate modalities or cross-modal self-verification by scoring the model's own outputs with another generated modality. Modus demonstrates strong out-of-the-box performance and is competitive with specialist and multitask baselines using a single model across various benchmarks. All materials are open-sourced at https://modus-multimodal.epfl.ch/.

</details>


### [73] [Knowledge-Guided Multimodal Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Video-Level Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25961)
*Podakanti Satyajith Chary,Barath Parthiban,Pranesh Velmurugan,Adeeba Khan,Nagarajan Ganapathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种多模态识别犹豫和矛盾情绪（A/H）的方法PRISM-AH，可有效提升对视频中A/H状态的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别健康行为改变前的犹豫和矛盾（A/H）对于及时干预至关重要，但A/H常体现在面部、声音、语言和身体等多模态且个体差异大，传统方法难以准确识别。

Method: PRISM-AH框架冻结视觉、音频和文本编码器，将多模态输入按时间窗对齐，采用流式轻量模型对跨模态矛盾进行评分，预测下一个时间窗揭示犹豫信号，挖掘行为原型，并结合个体元数据。利用密集时间窗注释进行辅助监督，决策阈值针对宏观F1校准，同时引入知识引导型大语言模型，对结构化证据推理，验证有效提升后才与主模型后融合。

Result: PRISM-AH在525个公开标注视频的测试集上宏观F1达到0.6133，显著优于零样本基准0.2827。推理增益模型在大规模测试集上也能迁移验证。

Conclusion: 论文提出的PRISM-AH多模态冲突识别方法能显著提升对视频中犹豫与矛盾的检测能力，具备较强的泛化能力，对健康行为相关的情感状态识别具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are conflicting affective states that precede the delay or abandonment of health behaviour change. Recognition of A/H at the video level is difficult, since the signal arises from disagreement across and within facial, vocal, linguistic, and bodily modalities, and manifests differently across individuals. The proposed PRISM-AH (Predictive Reasoning over Interacting Streams for Multimodal Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition), is a framework that treats A/H as a multimodal conflict that unfolds over time. Frozen vision, audio, and text encoders are aligned into short time windows and passed to a lightweight streaming model that scores cross-modal dissonance, predicts each next window to expose a hesitation surprise signal, discovers behaviour prototypes, and is conditioned on participant metadata. Dense window-level annotations supervise the model as an auxiliary objective, and the decision threshold is calibrated for macro F1. A knowledge-guided large language model then reasons over structured evidence using the expert cue taxonomy of the dataset, and its verdict is fused late only when validation performance improves. On the labelled public test partition of 525 videos, PRISM-AH attains a macro F1 of 0.6133, compared to the reported zero-shot baseline of 0.2827. The reasoning gain is validated to transfer from validation to the larger test partition.

</details>


### [74] [LaP-Forensics: Latent-Pixel Consistency Guided Multimodal Reasoning for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25962)
*Can Wang,Yuhao Wang,Yushe Cao,Canran Xiao,Fei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaP-Forensics通过结合重建残差与传统视觉特征，有效提升生成图像取证能力，实验结果表现优越，但对文本解释可靠性仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的假图检测方法多依赖于表面外观，但随着生成技术进步，图像伪造更加隐蔽，导致只靠视觉特征的检测手段效果变差。该工作目标是通过引入生成模型重建残差，增强检测手段的判别能力。

Method: 提出LaP-Forensics多模态架构：首先使用冻住权重的Stable Diffusion DDIM做图像反演和重建，得到原图与重建图像的残差，并与原RGB图像分别编码；随后，Where-What-Why模块输出掩码与文本解释，并进行有监督微调和组级相对策略优化（GRPO）结合残差与文本结构信息进行奖励；最后，融合RGB与重建残差特征通过独立头判别图像真假。

Result: 在UniversalFakeDetect任务下跨生成器检测能力良好，并在SynthScars基准集上具备竞争力的伪造区域定位表现。进一步的消融和对照实验证明残差流对检测能力有较大提升。

Conclusion: LaP-Forensics融合生成模型残差与视觉语义，提升合成图像检测和定位性能。但自由文本说明的真实性、以及面对后处理时的可靠性仍有待提升。

Abstract: Recent generative models can produce images with few obvious visual artifacts, weakening detectors and explanations that rely only on surface appearance. We present LaP-Forensics, a multimodal framework that augments RGB semantics with reconstruction-based forensic evidence. A frozen Stable Diffusion DDIM inversion-reconstruction model provides a fixed reconstruction reference, and its residual map measures local compatibility with that reference. Independent projectors encode the RGB image and residual map before a structured Where-What-Why model predicts a textual analysis and an artifact mask.Supervised fine-tuning is followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), whose reward combines mask overlap with output-structure and evidence-reference terms. These text-side terms encourage the model to refer to the consistency map but do not constitute a verifier of free-form textual truth. A separate image-level head fuses RGB and DDIM-residual class features. Experiments show cross-generator detection on UniversalFakeDetect and competitive artifact localization on the official SynthScars benchmark. Controlled cue-construction, inversion-horizon, component, reward-term, and counterfactual analyses support the utility of the residual stream under the evaluated settings, while free-form textual faithfulness and reliability under post-processing remain open limitations.

</details>


### [75] [Quasi-SVD: Learning a Lie-constrained matrix factorisation for real-time imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25967)
*Christopher Hahne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于GPU的Quasi-SVD分解框架，实现了全并行、可微的矩阵分解，显著提升了医学成像中的实时处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SVD算法在GPU上受限于串行计算，难以满足医学影像领域实时处理和在线临床部署的需求。

Method: 提出Quasi-SVD方法，对一组分解因子施加强正交性，另一组通过软约束近似，从而实现全并行GPU计算，无需迭代奇异向量优化。

Result: 在两类代表性医学影像任务上，Quasi-SVD较现有方法（如cuSOLVER与随机SVD）速度提升3-20倍，重建质量SSIM达到0.89-0.94，吞吐量超过25 FPS，远超当前经典解算器。

Conclusion: Quasi-SVD在保证重建精度基础上大幅提升了计算效率，推动了结构化矩阵分解在医学实时成像中的实用性。

Abstract: Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) underlies matrix factorisation tasks across computational imaging, with medical applications increasingly demanding real-time processing. Yet SVD algorithms are inherently sequential, constraining real-time GPU throughput and limit online deployment in clinical pipelines. This study introduces Quasi-SVD, a differentiable, fully parallelized matrix factorization framework for GPUs. Rather than enforcing orthogonality on both factors, it guarantees exact orthogonality for a single Lie-parameterized factor while recovering the remaining components through soft constraints, enabling efficient parallel decomposition without iterative singular-vector optimization. This asymmetric design, provably sufficient for valid factorisation, achieves reconstruction fidelity of SSIM = 0.89-0.94 and accelerates computation by 3-20x relative to cuSOLVER and randomised SVD, enabling throughput above 25 FPS. Performance is evaluated on two medical imaging tasks spanning complementary computational regimes: (1) spatio-temporal background subtraction for ultrasound localisation microscopy, requiring high-dimensional matrix separation, and (2) Mueller matrix polarimetry for neurosurgical tissue characterisation, requiring massive batch processing of small matrices. Across both regimes and multiple imaging instruments, the proposed framework demonstrates robust domain transfer and throughput exceeding 25 FPS at clinical matrix scales, a rate sufficient for live image-guided workflows that classical solvers cannot currently support in these settings. By prioritising downstream reconstruction fidelity over exact spectral recovery, Quasi-SVD makes structured matrix factorisation practical for real-time imaging.

</details>


### [76] [Schrödinger's Cat: Probabilistic Representation and Prediction of Potential Scene Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25984)
*Timy Phan,Jannik Wiese,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GARFIELD的新型概率模型，专门用于高效地预测场景中对象的未来运动分布，并能够快速采样多种可能的发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常只生成单一的预测视频或者采样少量未来轨迹，且未能明确建模所有可能运动的分布，因此无法全面刻画未来演化的不确定性与多样性。

Method: GARFIELD模型基于图像输入，并可选用时空稀疏的额外约束，学习结构化的时空潜在变量表示，对所有可能的运动分布进行建模。其核心是兼具高效联合轨迹采样和直接密度估算能力，通过高效确定性解码器获得底层运动分布。

Result: 实验表明GARFIELD能够在保持运动规划性能与大规模视频生成模型竞争的同时，实现高效的轨迹采样（提速97倍）和运动密度估算（比Monte-Carlo采样快100倍以上），支持交互式探索和不确定性感知的规划。

Conclusion: GARFIELD实现了快速且结构化的不确定未来运动建模，能本地化和细化场景中运动的不确定性，有望赋能更高效、智能的场景理解和规划任务。

Abstract: Predicting how a scene may evolve from partial observations requires reasoning about multiple possible futures rather than committing to a single trajectory. Existing approaches either generate appearance-dominated video predictions or sample a small number of trajectories without explicitly modeling the distribution of possible motion. We introduce Goal-Aware Representations of Future kInEmatic Latent Distributions (GARFIELD), a probabilistic model of scene kinematics that learns a structured spatio-temporal latent representation of the distribution over possible futures given an image and optional spatio-temporally sparse constraints. The same latent representation enables both joint sampling of all trajectories and direct access to the underlying motion distribution through an efficient deterministic density decoder. As a result, uncertainty about future motion can be localized to specific scene elements and timesteps and progressively refined through additional constraints. Experiments demonstrate strong motion planning performance competitive with large video generation models while sampling trajectories $97\times$ faster. Our method further estimates motion densities two orders of magnitude faster than Monte-Carlo sampling from motion generation models, enabling interactive exploration and uncertainty-aware planning.

</details>


### [77] [On the Use of Synthetic Data for Threshold Calibration in Face Recognition: Performance and Security Implications for Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25990)
*Arto Apila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文研究了在欧洲边境管控系统中，利用合成人脸数据进行门槛校准时的有效性及局限性。结果发现，虽然合成数据在受控环境下具有一定参考价值，但在复杂真实场景下表现显著下降，最终认为高安全领域还需依赖真实数据校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着欧洲引入大规模生物识别验证系统EES，需要极低错误匹配率的人脸识别技术。法规虽规定了中央系统的性能目标，但未明确成员国级别如何实际校准阈值，而真实数据因法规和实际限制难以获取。因此，作者希望探索以合成数据替代真实数据进行校准的可行性。

Method: 作者分析了合成与真实数据下，验证分数（真正与伪装样本分数）分布的一致性，并考察了用合成数据校准得到的门槛在跨领域（合成到真实间）的可迁移性，特别关注低 FMR 情形。

Result: 结果表明，合成数据在受控环境下基本能模拟校准表现，但因分数分布尾部不匹配，无法泛化到无约束环境，导致识别性能大幅下降且更易受到变形攻击。同时发现校准结果对具体数据集高度敏感。

Conclusion: 合成数据对系统开发和初步校准有帮助，但在高安全场景下，可靠的阈值选择和验证仍需基于代表实际环境的真实数据。

Abstract: The recently deployed Entry/Exit System (EES) introduces large-scale biometric verification into European border control, requiring face recognition systems to operate at extremely low false match rates (FMR). While regulatory frameworks define performance targets at the EES Central System level, they do not specify how verification thresholds should be calibrated in practice at the Member State level. In operational settings, obtaining representative real-world data for calibration is often constrained by legal, logistical, and privacy limitations.
  In this work, we investigate the use of synthetic face data for threshold calibration in document-to-live verification scenarios relevant to border control systems. We analyze the alignment of genuine and impostor score distributions between synthetic and real datasets and evaluate the transferability of calibrated thresholds across domains, with a focus on low-FMR operating points.
  Our results show that synthetic data can approximate calibration behavior in controlled settings, but fails to reliably generalize to unconstrained conditions due to mismatches in score distribution tails. These discrepancies lead to significant degradation in recognition performance and increased vulnerability to morph-based attacks. We further demonstrate that calibration outcomes are highly dataset-dependent, even across synthetic datasets.
  Overall, our findings highlight that while synthetic data is useful for system development and preliminary calibration, our results indicate that reliable threshold selection in high-security deployments typically requires validation and adjustment using representative real-world data.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Zooming: Learning Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25993)
*Fengxiang Wang,Jiangnan Huang,Mingshuo Chen,Yueying Li,Yang Shi,Junwei Luo,Haoyu Wang,Yansheng Li,Jing Zhang,Haiyan Zhao,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对超高分辨率遥感图像的多工具视觉推理数据集GeoMTVR和新的多工具视觉推理模型GeoLens，通过强化学习优化关键工具使用决策，提升了大模型在复杂视觉任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于超高分辨率遥感图像中的任务相关证据通常稀疏且分散，现有多模态大模型仅依赖局部放大（zoom-in）工具难以解决需全球搜索、多区域比较、路径规划等复杂任务。因此，作者设计了新的数据集和方法，以突破这一局限。

Method: 作者构建了包含1.3万条问题和推理轨迹的GeoMTVR数据集，支持多种视觉工具及跨工具证据整合。同时提出强化学习算法，聚焦于何时调用、选择何种工具、在何处应用、如何解释工具输出等关键决策。最后，通过有监督微调和强化学习联合训练出GeoLens模型。

Result: 实验表明，GeoLens在准确率、证据定位能力和工具使用效率等方面，均优于直接推理和单一工具放大策略。

Conclusion: 针对超高分辨率遥感图像的复杂推理任务，利用多工具协同和工具决策优化能显著提升多模态大模型的推理效果。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote-sensing (RS) imagery provides fine-grained Earth-observation evidence over city-scale scenes, but poses a fundamental challenge for multimodal large language models (MLLMs): task-relevant evidence is often sparse, local, and spatially dispersed across extremely large visual contexts. A natural solution is to equip MLLMs with zoom-in tools for active local inspection. However, through a pilot study on XLRS-Bench, we find that zoom-in is only partially effective: it resolves easy and medium-level tasks with locally recoverable evidence, but saturates on hard cases requiring global search, multi-region comparison, path planning, or dispersed-evidence reasoning. Motivated by this finding, we move beyond single-tool zoom-in and introduce GeoMTVR, a large-scale Geospatial Multi-Tool Visual Reasoning dataset built from wide-area satellite imagery. GeoMTVR contains 13K UHR VQA samples with interleaved reasoning trajectories, diverse visual tool calls, and returned visual observations, enabling models to learn question decomposition, tool selection, regional inspection, object-level grounding, auxiliary visual reasoning, and cross-tool evidence integration. Beyond supervised fine-tuning, we propose a tool-attention-focused reinforcement learning algorithm that concentrates optimization on critical tool-use decisions, including when to invoke tools, which tool to select, where to apply it, and how to interpret tool outputs. By combining SFT on GeoMTVR with our RL algorithm, we develop GeoLens, a multi-tool visual reasoning MLLM for UHR RS. Experiments show that GeoLens consistently outperforms direct reasoning and single-tool zoom-in baselines, achieving stronger accuracy, better evidence grounding, and more efficient tool-use trajectories.

</details>


### [79] [Parallel Decoding Distillation for Fast Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26004)
*Neta Shaul,Chao Liu,Arash Vahdat,Julius Berner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Parallel Decoding Distillation（PDD）的新方法，用于加速视频扩散和流匹配模型的生成过程，在保持高质量的前提下，大幅度提升生成速度和输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频扩散/流模型生成速度慢，因为采样过程迭代且计算量大。虽然主流的加速方法可以训练出少步生成器，但优化难度高且常常导致模式崩溃，影响视频的多样性和动态。

Method: 提出了一种基于轨迹的简化可扩展蒸馏方法PDD，每次推理可并行完成多个去噪步骤，无需传统求导或有限差分。同时，PDD架构和训练方式兼容现有的预训练模型，支持不同步数采样。

Result: 在LTX-2.3、Wan 14B、Qwen-Image等主流数据集上，PDD使用4-8步推理即达到SOTA性能，并显著提升了生成视频的多样性。

Conclusion: PDD在加速视频扩散和流模型生成、提升多样性上取得突破，优化难度小，兼容性强，有望广泛应用于高效高质量的视频和图像生成。

Abstract: Generation in video diffusion or flow models is computationally expensive due to the slow and iterative sampling process. Current state-of-the-art (SOTA) acceleration methods heavily rely on variational score distillation (VSD) and adversarial losses to distill diffusion models into few-step generators. Albeit achieving high-quality video generation, these training losses are notoriously hard to optimize and suffer from mode collapse, leading to loss of video diversity and lack of motion. In this paper, we introduce Parallel Decoding Distillation (PDD), a simplified and scalable trajectory-based distillation method for fast inference of diffusion and flow matching models. Our architecture and training procedure are compatible with any pre-trained model and support sampling with a varying number of function evaluations (NFE). PDD accelerates generation by predicting multiple denoising steps per network evaluation. Conceptually, it learns a representation of the mean velocity without regressing its derivative using JVPs or finite-difference approximations. Our method achieves SOTA performance with 4-8 NFE on LTX-2.3 Text-to-Video/Audio, Wan 14B Text-to-Video, and Qwen-Image Text-to-Image. Moreover, PDD presents a significant improvement in generated video diversity.

</details>


### [80] [Wonder: Video World Model Done Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26037)
*Jiacong Xu,Hanwen Jiang,Zhixin Shu,Kalyan Sunkavalli,Vishal M. Patel,Yiqun Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Wonder，一个通用的视频世界模型，实现了实时的、可控的世界探索，并支持长时序视频生成与交互式相机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成模型多局限于短时序或缺乏精细交互控制，难以支持用户自由探索未知区域和多轮交互。作者希望突破这一局限，实现像玩虚拟世界般探索的能力。

Method: 1. 提出密集坐标域的相机条件控制方法，使模型能直接将相机运动当作视觉输入。2. 设计基于稀疏注意力的高效记忆机制，保证长时序下对历史信息的快速精确检索。3. 提出优化知识蒸馏流程，提高学生模型对控制信号的响应及多样性生成能力。

Result: Wonder能以16 FPS生成多样、分钟级的视频片段，同时保证长期几何、外观及动态一致性，还支持以视频为条件的实时重拍。

Conclusion: Wonder显著提升了视频生成的交互性、时长和保真度，为虚拟世界自由探索和视频重拍打开了新方向。

Abstract: We present Wonder, a general-purpose video world model for real-time, camera-controllable world exploration. Given an image or a conditional video, Wonder constructs a playable world where users can navigate interactively by moving the camera, discovering unseen regions, and revisiting previously observed areas in real time and over a long-term horizon. Achieving this capability requires a system-level co-design of control method, memory mechanism, and training strategy. We introduce a novel camera conditioning with a dense coordinate field whose renderings provide spatially aligned motion and orientation cues, allowing the model to interpret camera motion directly as visual evidence. To support fast and precise memory retrieval over a growing generation context, we propose an efficient sparse attention-based memory mechanism, enabling the model to selectively attend to a small set of relevant context tokens at inference time, regardless of actual context length. We further develop several techniques to rectify the self-forcing-style distillation pipeline, improving the student model's ability to respect control signals, as well as maintaining diverse generation modes and long-term memory from the teacher. Together, these components enable Wonder to synthesize diverse, minute-scale videos at 16 FPS while preserving coherent geometry, appearance, and dynamics across long rollouts. Beyond image-to-video generation, Wonder naturally supports video-conditioned generation, allowing existing dynamic scenes to be re-shot in real time.

</details>


### [81] [VetClaw: An Edge-Cloud Multimodal Agentic System for Veterinary Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26042)
*Syed Mhamudul Hasan,Anas AlSobeh,Hussein Zangoti,Abdur R. Shahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VetClaw是一款基于边缘-云架构的多模态智能系统，用于兽医疾病的早期筛查，通过图像和症状描述实现无监督疾病分类，并具备安全管理和失败处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前针对动物疾病的早期筛查大多依赖静态图像分类，智能化、流程化、安全性的能力有限，尤其是在现场场景中实时预警和工具调度不足。该研究旨在提升筛查的自动化水平、增强诊断安全性和多模态能力。

Method: VetClaw采用相机模块作为边缘感知设备，将采集到的图像和选填症状描述发送到云端视觉-语言模型，实现无监督/零样本疾病分类。系统架构上，将代理交互与流程编排分离：OpenClaw负责边缘端的工具调度与交互，LangGraph管理整体筛查流程，包括安全检测、失败恢复与日志记录。

Result: 实验表明，单独依赖图像的模型预测能力有限，而结合症状描述的多模态输入能有效提升无监督分类性能。该系统可以实时采集证据、调用外部模型、执行安全规则并推送诊断提醒。

Conclusion: VetClaw将静态的预测模型升级为可协调、有安全保障、可工具化和故障处理的智能系统，有效提高了兽医疾病早筛的智能化与可靠性。

Abstract: We present VetClaw, an edge-cloud multimodal agentic system for early veterinary disease screening. VetClaw uses a camera module as an edge sensing device and sends captured images, together with optional symptom descriptions, to a server-hosted vision-language model for zero-shot disease classification. The system separates agent interaction from workflow orchestration: OpenClaw provides scheduling, tool access, user interaction, and notification services on the edge device, while LangGraph manages the stateful screening workflow, including input validation, image transmission, model invocation, safety checks, conditional routing, failure handling, and structured logging. This design moves beyond static image classification by enabling the system to collect visual evidence, invoke external models, apply deterministic safety rules, and generate diagnostic-support alerts. Results show that image-only VLM prediction remains limited, whereas symptom-guided and multimodal inputs improve zero-shot classification performance. Thus, VetClaw transforms a static prediction model into a coordinated, safety-aware system that can use tools, manage workflows, handle failures, and escalate uncertain cases.

</details>

### [82] [TimeCapsule: Generative Hallucination as a Method for Historical Sensemaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24750)
*Hayk Grigorian,Hamed Yaghoobian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeCapsule，是专门用维多利亚时期（1800-1875）文本训练的1.2B参数语言模型，能更好地反映历史语境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型由于训练数据覆盖现代内容，对于历史事件或语境的再现并不可靠，因此需要时空“隔离”的模型来专注特定历史时期。

Method: 作者训练了一个1.2B参数的LLaMA风格模型TimeCapsule，只用维多利亚文本进行训练，并与GPT-2及更大现代模型在历史文本上的表现作对比。

Result: 在维多利亚散文的困惑度上，TimeCapsule比GPT-2基线降低了45.4%。虽然大模型困惑度更低，但缺乏时间隔离。定性分析显示，模型能对现代概念生成富有历史色彩的类比解释。两位人文学者在分辨真实与AI生成文本时，约40%的真实段落被误判为机器生成，显示“真实性危机”。

Conclusion: 把模型与未来信息隔离后，模型的“幻觉”可以成为理解19世纪思维方式的新途径，有助于探索历史知识与观念。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are temporally overexposed: trained on vast contemporary corpora, they encode present-day concepts that make them unreliable narrators of the past. We present TimeCapsule, a 1.2B-parameter LLaMA-style causal model trained exclusively on Victorian texts (1800-1875) as an epistemologically isolated generative archive. Quantitative evaluation shows a 45.4% perplexity reduction over a GPT-2 baseline on held-out Victorian prose, while larger contemporary causal models achieve lower raw perplexity through broader pretraining but lack temporal isolation. TimeCapsule exhibits computational sensemaking, generating historically plausible analogical explanations for unfamiliar modern concepts (e.g., describing a computer as a "hypertrophied lung"). A qualitative hermeneutic probe with two humanities scholars revealed a crisis of authenticity, as both misclassified approximately 40% of genuine Victorian excerpts as machine-produced. We argue that structural ignorance of the future transforms hallucinations into interpretive probes of nineteenth-century ontologies.

</details>


### [83] [Measuring and Improving Behavioral Consistency in Large Language Models through Fact-Heuristic-Emotion State Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24765)
*Gi-Hun Lee,Joong Yull Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为Cognitive Kernel Model（CKM）的提示级状态强制方法，用于提升大型语言模型（LLMs）决策一致性，无需修改模型权重。CKM要求模型在决策前将信息分为事实、推测和情感三种类型。实验表明，CKM显著降低了输出波动和决策反转率，但并未提升推理正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在面对相同决策问题时输出结果不稳定，甚至会因为自己的前次答案作为上下文时推翻先前决策。这种不一致性会影响实际应用的可靠性，因此需要探索无需更改模型权重情况下的改进方法。

Method: 本文提出了Cognitive Kernel Model（CKM），作为一种提示级状态管理层。要求模型在作决策前，将输入拆分为Fact（事实）、Heuristic（推测）及Emotion（情感）三个部分，迫使模型追踪决策所依据的信息类型。方法通过结构化状态管理并经过多个消融实验及温度控制实验进行评估。

Result: 1）CKM显著降低重复输出的变异性；2）状态持久性大幅减少决策反转率；3）这种效果不是单纯的JSON结构化造成；4）固定锚状态下的内在随机性几乎可以忽略；5）在高采样随机性下，CKM优势更为明显；6）增益分别来自结构性约束和内容分离两方面，CKM是唯一能既提升一致性又减少反转的方案。

Conclusion: 通过强制LLM区分事实、推测和情感，CKM能有效度量并改善决策一致性，但并不会提升推理正确性。因此行为一致性是可测量且可通过结构性管理手段部分提升的特性，这一发现对实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can give different answers to the same decision problem across runs, and reverse a decision when their own prior answer returns as context. We ask whether this instability can be measured and partially reduced without changing model weights.
  We test the Cognitive Kernel Model (CKM), a prompt-level state-enforcement layer. Before deciding, the model must separate its input into three epistemic roles: Fact (given or verifiable), Heuristic (inferred or assumed), and Emotion (evaluative or priority signal). CKM adds no capability; it forces the model to track what kind of information it uses before acting. Formally it maintains a structured state S_t = {F_t, H_t, E_t} updated by a transition function.
  We evaluate CKM on Korean-language decision scenarios (ambiguity, ethical conflict, resource allocation, error handling) across 26 LLMs from four vendors and 37,403 observations, via four core experiments, a 4-arm ablation, a 5-arm sham-restriction ablation, and a temperature probe. Findings:
  (1) CKM reduces repeated-output variability (random-effects Hedges' g=1.09, 95% CI [0.83, 1.35], 31 model pairs);
  (2) state persistence cuts the decision-flip rate by 82% in newer models (g=1.52);
  (3) the effect is not JSON formatting alone (value-only recomputation, g=2.24);
  (4) intrinsic randomness under fixed anchor states is negligible;
  (5) the advantage grows under sampling stochasticity (g=2.87 at temperature 0.7);
  (6) a sham ablation attributes about 45% of the gain to structural scaffolding and 55% to Fact/Heuristic/Emotion content, and CKM is the only arm that both raises consistency and reduces flipping.
  CKM does not improve reasoning correctness. The narrower result: behavioral consistency is measurable, varies across models, and is partially improvable by forcing models to separate facts, assumptions, and evaluative signals before deciding.

</details>


### [84] [Neuromorphic Diffusion Language Models: Addressing Compute and Memory Bottlenecks via Sparsity and Block Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24841)
*Dengyu Wu,Clement Ruah,Jiechen Chen,Bipin Rajendran,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出神经形态掩码扩散语言模型（N-MDLMs），结合区块扩散和脉冲神经形态计算，显著提升大型语言模型推理的能效和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归大型语言模型（AR-LLMs）在推理阶段效率低，每生成一个 token 都需全部参数参与，导致能耗高、访存瓶颈显著。已有的掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）虽能并行生成多个 token，部分缓解内存瓶颈，但在计算受限情形下优势有限。因此亟需模型结构创新，进一步提升在现代高芯片内存平台下的推理能效。

Method: 提出 N-MDLMs，将区块扩散机制与脉冲式神经形态计算结合。区块扩散实现每次参数访问生成多个 token，提升吞吐量。脉冲计算为空间激活稀疏提供硬件友好处理，跳过无效信道，减少实际参数读取与计算。同时构建基于 Roofline 原理的 token 级分析模型，量化扩散与稀疏性联合对推理效率的提升。

Result: 实验表明，N-MDLMs 在翻译任务上相较传统 AR-LLMs 和 MDLMs 推理效率显著提升，能效更高，即使在计算受限平台也表现优越；特别是因脉冲稀疏机制降低了有效计算和参数传输。

Conclusion: N-MDLMs 通过稀疏扩散并联机制，突破现有模型在推理能效与吞吐的瓶颈，为神经形态实现的高效语言模型指明新方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) large language models (LLMs) are inherently inefficient at inference time because each generated token requires accessing the full set of model parameters, leading to low operational intensity and high energy consumption. Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) partially address this limitation for memory-bound settings by allowing multiple tokens to be generated per parameter access. In order to further enhance inference efficiency on modern platforms with extensive in-chip memory, this work proposes neuromorphic MDLMs (N-MDLMs), which integrate block diffusion with spike-based neuromorphic computation to jointly improve throughput and energy efficiency. While block diffusion increases token throughput by producing multiple tokens per parameter access, spike-induced sparsity reduces effective parameter traffic and computations by skipping inactive channels. To analyze the synergistic effect of sparsity and diffusion, we develop a token-level roofline-inspired model that captures the combined impact of block-parallel generation and spike sparsity on decoding efficiency. Experimental results on translation tasks show that, thanks to spike-induced sparsity, N-MDLMs achieve substantial improvements in energy efficiency and throughput even in compute-bound platforms for which MDLMs would fail to improve over AR-LLMs.

</details>


### [85] [Research Report on Noise-Shaped One-Bit Coefficients in Discrete Polynomial Fourier Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24868)
*Shengquan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种在归一化离散多项式Fourier扩展中利用噪声整形一位系数的新方法，分析了ΣΔ量化器下的误差衰减率，并推广到高阶、光滑权重、以及多维情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字信号处理中，离散Fourier扩展的高效准确逼近对噪声和量化误差非常敏感，因此需要理论分析误差行为，更好地利用低比特(如一位)量化结果提升数值方法的稳定性和效率。

Method: 首先针对一阶ΣΔ量化器推导出误差表达和界限，再通过离散分部求和分析误差随多项式阶数N的变化，并对更高阶ΣΔ量化、多项式相位、多维参数等情况进行了理论推广，提出了端点修正和兼容性条件。

Result: 一阶ΣΔ噪声整形误差随N衰减速率为$O(N^{-1})$，在适用于抛物线相位和紧致参数集的输入类上已达到最优；高阶情形下，在满足光滑性或Holder连续性权重下可分别实现$O(N^{-r})$和$O(N^{-(r-1+α)})$的衰减速率。还证明了$L^2$正交关系、高阶矩公式、核局部估计及振荡传输界等结果。

Conclusion: 噪声整形ΣΔ量化在离散多项式Fourier扩展中的理论误差界经本报告系统建立，证明了其在低比特模拟处理中的有效性和高阶推广潜力，对稳定高效数值逼近和信号分析具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: This report studies noise-shaped one-bit coefficients in normalized discrete polynomial Fourier extension. For first-order Sigma-Delta quantization, the error is written as $e_k=u_k-q_k=Δv_k$ with a uniformly bounded state. Discrete summation by parts then yields variation estimates for complex weights and an $O(N^{-1})$ approximation rate on compact parameter sets. For the parabolic phase $φ_{x,t}(ξ)=xξ+tξ^2$, the bound is expressed through $J(x,t)=\int_0^1 |x+2tξ|dξ$, and the uniform $N^{-1}$ rate is shown to be sharp over the admissible input class. Higher-order finite-record identities are derived with all endpoint traces retained. Under endpoint compatibility, or after explicit boundary correction, an $r$th-order noise-shaped error $e=Δ^r v$ gives $O(N^{-r})$ decay for sufficiently smooth weights and $O(N^{-(r-1+α)})$ decay for $C^{r-1,α}$ weights. Exact $L^2$ orthogonality identities, fourth-moment formulas, local kernel estimates, and oscillatory transfer bounds are also established. Extensions to polynomial phases, multidimensional parameter families, growing observation regions, and correlated state models are included.

</details>


### [86] [DS@GT ARC at CheckThat! 2026: LLM-Based Trace Ranking and Grouped Reward Modeling for Multilingual Numerical Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25069)
*Sagnik Sinha,Shreyas Shrestha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并比较了两种自动验证数值性声明的方法，重点在用LLM对推理溯源排序及终判决预测，涵盖英语和阿拉伯语。LLM微调法整体优于基于特征的轻量模型，阿拉伯语下AraBERT又优于通用多语模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值性声明的自动核查需要结合语言理解与定量推理，目前相关任务复杂且精准度有限。CLEF 2026 CheckThat!任务推动了该领域的发展，亟需更高效准确的解决方案。

Method: 提出两类方案：一是对LLM verifier用LoRA微调，对每条推理路径做二分类评分，并用Best-of-N选最终判决。另则采用轻量TF-IDF模型结合数字和时间特征打分，再按类别聚合。还尝试了子声明分解。阿语部分将通用多语模型与AraBERT做比较。

Result: LLM微调方法在大多数指标上，尤其Recall@5优于轻量reward模型。reward模型对“Conflicting”类更强。声明分割没带来提升反而增噪。阿语实验里AraBERT优于多语基线。

Conclusion: 微调LLM能更有效排序推理并判别数值声明，AraBERT对阿语适应性更好。分割声明对效果无益。以后可进一步优化推理排序和多语种特化。

Abstract: Automated verification of numerical claims is a challenging problem, as it requires both language understanding and quantitative reasoning. This paper describes our system for CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Task 2, which focuses on ranking reasoning traces generated by large language models (LLMs) and predicting a final verdict for numerical claims in English and Arabic. We explore two approaches. The first approach fine-tunes an LLM-based verifier using LoRA to score each reasoning trace independently as a binary classification problem, and selects the final verdict using Best-of-N selection. We further experiment with adaptive sub-claim decomposition to break complex claims into simpler parts before verification. The second approach uses a lightweight TF-IDF reward model with handcrafted numeric and temporal overlap features to score traces, and aggregates scores by verdict group to determine the final prediction. For Arabic, we compare a general multilingual model against AraBERT, a language-specific model pretrained on Arabic text. Our results show that the LLM-based approach outperforms the lightweight reward model on most metrics, particularly Recall@5, while the reward-based approach shows stronger performance on the Conflicting class. Sub-claim decomposition did not improve performance, suggesting that claim splitting introduces noise rather than aiding reasoning. For Arabic, AraBERT outperforms the multilingual baseline across most metrics.

</details>


### [87] [Evaluating Communicative Belief Updates in Large Language Models via Implicature Recognition and Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25094)
*Cesare Spinoso-Di Piano,Verna Dankers,Marius Mosbach,Jackie Chi Kit Cheung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并公开了第一个专注于含义撤销的专家标注数据集，用以评价大语言模型（LLM）对未明说信念及其变化的理解能力，结果显示LLM在人类层次上依然存在明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类语言交流依赖于隐含信念及信念的动态更新，若要提升LLM和用户之间的自然交流能力，就需要精确建模这些隐含成分。然而，目前关于LLM是否具备此项能力的研究仍不充分。

Method: 作者创建了一个包含隐含意义及其撤销的专家标注数据集，并通过众包收集对应的人类判断，系统评估了LLM识别与理解含义更新（如通过含义撤销现象实现的信念变化）的表现。同时，通过对不同类型和形式的撤销进行对照实验，探究LLM对信念更新任务成功或失败的原因。

Result: 结果显示，LLM在理解含义撤销和信念更新方面明显落后于人类，特别是在自然场景下。此外，控制实验发现，LLM部分成功可能依赖于先验知识，而失败则与具体撤销类型和表达方式有关。

Conclusion: 当前LLM尚未达到人类在未明说信念及其更新理解上的水平，需要进一步改进模型在语用推理和信念建模方面的能力。

Abstract: Human language is driven by unspoken beliefs and belief updates, making these critical to model for successful communication between large language models (LLMs) and their users. In this paper, we evaluate the ability of LLMs to recognize unspoken beliefs made through implicatures and to understand their updates through implicature cancellation: the pragmatic phenomenon whereby an utterance's implied meaning is weakened or negated. We create the first expert-annotated implicature cancellation dataset, [DatasetName], crowdsourced for human judgements of implicatures and their corresponding cancellations. We find that LLM belief update understanding lags behind that of humans, especially in more naturally-occurring scenarios. Additional control experiments suggest that successes in LLM belief updates may stem in part from a reliance on prior beliefs, and that failures in belief updates may depend on their type and on their form. Overall, our study suggests that current LLMs have not yet reached human-level understanding of unspoken beliefs and belief updates. Code and data are available at https://github.com/cesare-spinoso/ImplicatureX.

</details>


### [88] [TabRank: Chain-of-Thought Distillation for Table Re-Rankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25182)
*Adarsh Singh,Kushal Raj Bhandari,Jianxi Gao,Soham Dan,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TabRank，一个用于表格检索（Tabular Retrieval）的推理重排序框架，并显著提升了多种表格问答数据集上的检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着结构化信息检索任务中，如何高效、精准地根据问题检索相关表格变得越来越重要。传统的稀疏或密集检索模型在语义理解和推理能力上有限，促使学界寻求更强的重排序方法，尤其是神经网络和大语言模型驱动的推理重排序。

Method: 作者首先构建了一个包含6728条推理链数据的表格重排序数据集。提出两种训练紧凑型推理重排序模型的策略：（1）显式chain-of-thought（CoT）推理蒸馏；（2）通过提示将教师模型的推理链条件化输入给学生重排序模型。此外，对TabRank在多个异构领域和多表格场景下进行了外部分布泛化测试。

Result: TabRank方法在多个表格检索数据集上显著提升性能，如HybridQA提升30.5%、SQA提升15.2%、TabFact提升52.9%、TATQA提升13.1%（基于Acc@10指标，相比基础模型）。TabRank还展现出良好的多表格推理泛化能力。

Conclusion: TabRank基于推理的重排序方法，有效提升了表格检索的质量和泛化性。其代码、数据和模型已开源，便于社区复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: The ability to retrieve relevant tables for answering questions is a key task for structured information retrieval. Multi-stage retrieval systems rely heavily on rerankers to refine candidate lists produced by efficient first-stage retrievers. As a result, neural rerankers and LLM-based reranking methods have become increasingly important due to their superior capacity for semantic understanding and reasoning compared to conventional sparse or dense retrieval models. Recently, Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) equipped with explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning have shown strong improvements in ranking quality in unstructured passage retrieval. In this work, we present TabRank, a framework for training reasoning rerankers for Tabular Retrieval. We first present a comprehensive dataset of 6728 reasoning traces for tabular reranking on the Natural Questions Tables dataset. We then explore two variants of training a compact reasoning model on these reasoning traces: explicit CoT distillation and conditioning the student reranker on the teacher's reasoning trace within the prompt. We stress-test TabRank on several out-of-distribution generalization settings on diverse domains and multi-table scenarios. Our approach significantly improves performance across a variety of table retrieval datasets, increasing Acc@10 by 30.5% on HybridQA, 15.2% on SQA, 52.9% on TabFact, and 13.1% on TATQA subsets of the Multi-Table QA Benchmark compared to the base model. Notably, TabRank generalizes effectively to multi-table reasoning. Our code, data and models are available at https://github.com/AdarshSingh7647/TabRanker

</details>


### [89] [A Cross-lingual Comparison of Human and Classification Model Entrainment Behavior in Code-switched Speech Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25202)
*Debasmita Bhattacharya,Siying Ding,Alayna Nguyen,Julia Hirschberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了普通话-英语、印地语-英语和西班牙语-英语对话中的跨语言对话配合现象，发现词汇层面的互动具有普适性，但语音-韵律和代码转换风格存在上下文差异。同时分析了分类模型能否反映这种人类行为，并发现模型捕捉能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话配合（entrainment）主要研究单语或书面语，口语代码转换（如中英夹杂）下的配合几乎未被关注。多语对话需求增长，有必要了解机器如何处理此类复杂交流。

Method: 对普通话-英语、印地语-英语和西班牙语-英语的口语对话开展跨语言配合分析。分别从词汇、语音-韵律和代码转换风格等层面细致评估，并用特征重要性及消融实验考察经典及Transformer模型在配合检测方面的表现。

Result: 词汇配合普遍存在，但其他层面（如语音-韵律、代码转换风格）随具体语境变动明显。模型能一定程度检测对话配合，但它们关注的特征与人类重视的并不一致。

Conclusion: 提出了基于人类行为的模型决策评估方法，为后续构建能自然交流、多语风格代码切换的对话系统指明了新挑战。

Abstract: Conversational entrainment is well-studied in monolingual and written contexts, but remains underexplored in spoken code-switching (CSW). We present a novel cross-lingual analysis of entrainment in Mandarin-English, Hindi-English, and Spanish-English dialogue and show that, while lexical entrainment generalizes across language pairs, entrainment over acoustic-prosodic and CSW style aspects exhibits context-specific variation. We build on these findings by asking whether classification models capture these human behavioral patterns. Applying feature importance and ablation analyses, we find that classical and Transformer-based classifiers detect entrainment reasonably well but consistently prioritize features other than those most salient to human entraining behavior. Our approach introduces a human-grounded framework for evaluating model decision-making in multilingual stylistic contexts, and suggests future challenges for developing conversational agents capable of producing naturalistic code-switched speech.

</details>


### [90] [Where Steering Signals Come From: Activation Source Selection in Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25270)
*Jiaran Ye,Lingxu Ran,Zijun Yao,Chenpeng Wang,Yong Jiang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文分析了激活引导（activation steering）中，信号源的选择对大语言模型操控效果的重要影响，并提出了优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然激活引导是当前操控语言模型输出的重要方法，但此前对操控信号来源的选择关注较少。本研究希望揭示：信号的具体来源（即激活源选择）对引导效果有多大、信号源的最优选择机制，以及能否进一步增强信号有效性。

Method: 作者在三种指令微调模型和四类引导任务中，对激活引导过程中的信号来源（即源上下文和激活读取策略的组合）进行了系统比较，并提出了tail subtraction新方法以优化信号质量。

Result: 实验发现，尽管下游干预操作保持不变，仅仅改变激活信号的来源就足以极大改变引导的成功率。有效的引导信号往往不是单纯出现在目标文本中，而是出现在模型即将进入或继续目标行为的“执行边界”状态。tail subtraction方法能消除无关语义，获得更稳定、干净的引导信号。

Conclusion: 研究表明，激活引导的有效性依赖于模型即将执行的行为表征，而不仅仅是已出现内容。tail subtraction方法为获取优质引导信号提供了新途径，对后续相关研究和模型操控技术有指导意义。

Abstract: Activation steering controls language models by adding vectors or features to hidden states at inference time, but the upstream source of these steering signals is often treated as a secondary detail. We study this source choice as activation source selection: the combination of source context and activation readout policy used to collect the hidden states from which a steering signal is built. Holding the downstream intervention fixed, we show across three instruction-tuned models and four steering task families that changing only the source activations substantially changes steering success. We further find that effective steering is not explained simply by whether the desired behavior appears in the source text. Instead, strong signals come from execution-boundary states, where the model is about to produce or continue the target behavior. This pre-/post-realization distinction explains why answer-based sources sometimes work: their useful component aligns with execution-boundary directions rather than target appearance alone. Building on this view, we introduce tail subtraction, which removes shared prompt and continuation semantics from boundary states and yields cleaner, more stable steering signals. Overall, our results suggest that steering depends on representations of what the model is about to do, not merely on what has already appeared.

</details>


### [91] [CoSA: Accelerating Long-Context Inference via Proxy-Kernel Co-Designed Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25291)
*Yufei Xue,Lin Niu,Hong Liu,Siran Liu,Hanyong Shao,Wei Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoSA的新型稀疏注意力方法，大幅提升了长上下文推理的速度，且在较低运算预算下仍能保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自注意力机制计算复杂度为二次方，导致处理超长上下文时计算成本极高。虽然已有稀疏化近似方法（如proxy-based block-sparse attention）能缓解此问题，但在资源极为紧张时会显著牺牲模型准确性，无法有效支撑实际需求。

Method: CoSA为一种训练无关的两阶段稀疏注意力方案，耦合Kernel-Aware Proxy（KAP）与Ordered-Skipping Kernel（OSK）：1）KAP在适中预算下优先选择重要的块，并生成有序mask，定义KV读取顺序；2）OSK内核利用此顺序，并可结合动态在线统计进一步跳过更多块，以适应更严格的预算。

Result: 在主流大语言模型和长上下文基准上，CoSA在更低预算下取得更高准确率，实现了近5倍的注意力计算加速和2.5倍以上的首token延迟缩短，同时性能基本无损。

Conclusion: CoSA方法克服了传统稀疏注意力在超低预算下准确率下滑的问题，实现了高效且几乎无损的长上下文推理，具有明显的实际应用价值。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of self-attention makes long-context inference prohibitively expensive, and proxy-based block-sparse attention has become a practical remedy. Existing methods typically rely on a proxy to predict a binary sparse mask and a kernel to consume this mask and perform sparse attention computation. Such an approach is effective under moderate budgets. However, as the budget tightens, the estimated proxy inevitably drops some salient blocks, while the kernel can only apply the sparse mask mechanically, leading to an evident drop in model accuracy. We propose CoSA, a two-stage training-free Sparse Attention under proxy-kernel CO-design, which couples a Kernel-Aware Proxy (KAP) with an Ordered-Skipping Kernel (OSK). In the first stage, the KAP selects blocks under a moderate budget and produces an ordered mask that prescribes the order in which KV pages are visited in the kernel inner loop. In the second stage, the OSK applies this mask and skips more blocks under a tightened budget given online-softmax statistics. Across mainstream LLM backbones and long-context benchmarks, CoSA attains higher accuracy at lower budgets. Impressively, CoSA achieves a 4.93$\times$ attention speedup and reduces end-to-end Time-to-First-Token by 2.53$\times$ under a context length of 128K with negligible performance degradation.

</details>


### [92] [Toward a systematic method for identifying language areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25305)
*Hiram Ring*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于地理聚类的新方法，用于识别语言宏区或较小的语言接触区，实现更系统化的地域语言划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往语言宏区（macroareas）的划分主要依赖专家判断和地理大陆的划分，缺乏系统方法，而宏区在语言类型学研究中对于控制语言接触和历史遗传的重要性突出，因此需要一种更系统的划分手段。

Method: 作者提出并应用了一种简单的地理聚类方法，根据地理位置对语言进行分组，从而识别任何区域的语言群组。

Result: 该方法得出的全球宏区大致与现有分组相符，同时能够识别某些著名的语言联盟（sprachbund）中的本地群组。

Conclusion: 提出的方法为识别语言接触区提供了系统工具，可克服现有基于主观专家判断的不足，有助于语言类型学里更加客观和精细地划分语言地理区域。

Abstract: Macroareas are geographical areas used in typological research for grouping variables of interest. In linguistic typology, languages in a given macroarea are considered to have potential for contact, in contrast to those outside the area, where contact is less likely. Along with language family membership, macroareas are used as controls for models in linguistic typology, in an attempt to address the problem of autocorrelation - the observation that historical developments or typological patterns may be due to contact between neighboring languages and/or inheritance from a common ancestral language. Macroareas are therefore a central aspect of research that seeks to separate universal properties of language from local (or language-specific) properties. Existing macroareas largely depend on expert determinations of what constitutes a geographical area of potential contact, and to date have mainly aligned with continents or landmasses (Hammarström and Donohue 2014; Nichols, Witzlack-Makarevich, and Bickel 2013). While there are various historical and theoretical reasons for these groupings, there as of yet has been no systematic approach to identifying such areas for a given region. This paper attempts to address such a gap and move beyond macroarea to identification of language areas of relatively arbitrary size, presenting a simple geographical clustering method for identifying groupings over any area. The method produces a set of worldwide macroareas that largely align with existing groupings, as well as local groupings for a well-known sprachbund.

</details>


### [93] [CAST: Game Solvers as Turn-Level Teachers for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25308)
*Yu Wang,Yi-Kai Zhang,Wentao Shi,Ziang Ye,Yuchun Miao,Yueqing Sun,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Lan-Zhe Guo,Han-Jia Ye,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的奖励信号生成方法，通过借助游戏求解器的状态价值变化，为大语言模型训练中的回合决策提供更细粒度的激励，显著提升了模型在多种游戏和环境中的泛化和零样本表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在需要长程决策的任务（如策略类游戏）中，通常利用稀疏的最终奖励进行强化学习，但这导致信号过于稀缺，难以精确追踪并强化关键决策。需要一种更密集且易实现的中间反馈方法。

Method: 作者观察到游戏求解器的状态价值变动可反映每步动作对成功的推动程度，进而提出CAST方法：将这些价值变化转化为“solver advantage”，并将其作为逐步奖励信号融入强化学习中。相较于以往需要教师模型logit的方法，CAST仅需标量数值，可低成本实现。

Result: 在Sokoban、Minesweeper、Rush Hour等游戏以及ALFWorld和WebShop任务上，CAST在域内和未见难度测试中均优于所有对比基线，而且在零样本泛化测试中获得了最高平均分数。

Conclusion: 通过借助求解器状态价值变化，CAST为回合级奖励分配提供了实用、高效的新途径，有效提升了大语言模型决策任务的训练效率和泛化能力。该方法具有通用性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) to act in long-horizon games is a promising step toward generalist decision-making, yet reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) relies on sparse final rewards that reveal little about which decisions determine success. Denser process signals could supply this missing turn-level credit, but existing sources are hard to keep both cheap and accurate. We observe that changes in a game solver's state value reveal whether an action advances the state toward success. Building on this insight, we propose CAST (Credit Assignment from Solver Teachers), which converts these value changes into solver advantages and injects them into RLVR as turn-level signals. We further show that, under a soft-optimal solver assumption, maximizing the solver advantage is equivalent to on-policy distillation from the solver, requiring only scalar values rather than teacher logits. Across Sokoban, Minesweeper, and Rush Hour, CAST outperforms all trained baselines on every game under both in-domain and unseen-difficulty evaluation and achieves the highest average zero-shot performance on ALFWorld and WebShop. Our code is available at https://github.com/Wloner0809/CAST.

</details>


### [94] [Every Time I Hire a Linguist, Inference Costs Go Down: On Linguistic Rules as Effective Prompt Compressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25335)
*Jianfei Ma,Zhaoxin Feng,Emmanuele Chersoni,Si Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅依赖语言学规则的LLM提示词压缩方法，避免了运行时的LM得分计算，压缩效果与先进方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的LLM提示词压缩方法需通过语言模型前向传播来评分，每次压缩都要消耗大量计算资源，效率低下。鉴于语言学长期关注信息筛选，作者探讨能否仅用语言学规则实现高效压缩。

Method: 作者基于词汇、句法、语义及篇章多级信号，通过离线进化搜索，发现有效的规则组合用于提示压缩。该方法在部署时无需LM推理，仅用CPU本地规则处理；评估时采用双通道实验，兼顾压缩和还原准确性。

Result: 在短文、多文档推理和对话记忆QA等任务上，所提出的规则压缩器性能接近当前先进的提示词压缩策略。轻度至中度压缩效果最佳，压缩更激进时性能下降，同时压缩和还原路径表现有差异。

Conclusion: 高效提示词压缩可通过语言学多层级规则融合实现。随着压缩率提高，压缩策略由逐词剪除向整句抽取转变，无需LM推理即可实现高性价比压缩。

Abstract: Prompt compression shortens LLM input to reduce inference cost, yet existing methods score token importance through LM forward passes. It remains questionable whether such nuanced, costly token selection is necessary. Compression requires identifying informative content, a problem that linguistic research has long addressed through cues that can be operationalized as deterministic rules. We therefore ask: can \textbf{linguistic rules alone} serve as effective prompt compressors, without LM-based scoring at compression time?
  To address this, we conduct offline evolutionary search over lexical, syntactic, semantic, and discourse seeds to find competitive rule combinations. The resulting linguistic compressor requires no LM forward pass at deployment and uses only CPU-side processing for compression. We evaluate it with a dual-path protocol to balance compression quality and reconstruction fidelity.
  Across short passages, multi-document reasoning, and dialogue-memory QA datasets, evolved compressors achieve performance similar to that of recent advanced prompt-compression strategies. Performance is strongest under light-to-moderate compression and degrades as compression becomes more aggressive, while the Direct and Reconstruction paths exhibit distinct patterns. Evolutionary analysis reveals that effective compression fuses signals across linguistic levels and, as the compression ratio increases, rules shift from token pruning to sentence extraction.

</details>


### [95] [Temporal-Distance JEPA: Plan-Aware Representation Learning for Latent World Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25337)
*Jiaxin Bai,Jiaxuan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了TD-JEPA，一种改进的联合嵌入预测架构，能够更有效地用于基于世界模型的规划，通过从无奖励轨迹中挖掘时间距离作为规划代价，提升离线日志中的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统JEPA方法优化的是短期的潜在表示预测，但现实规划任务需要对多步未来进行排名。以往方法多用潜在空间的欧式距离作为进展评估标准，但该度量并非来自规划目标而是嵌入学习的副产品，未必能有效衡量任务进展。为解决这一问题，作者希望挖掘更符合任务推进结构的规划代价。

Method: 提出TD-JEPA算法，保留LeWM的编码器-预测器结构，同时通过轨迹的时间顺序挖掘有向的时间距离作为监督信号：同一轨迹内按顺序的步骤作为正样本，不同轨迹之间配对作为负样本，并通过rollout一致性项对齐规划步长。这些有监督信号既作为实际部署时的规划成本，也提升了欧式空间中规划表现。

Result: 在Two-Room任务用该方法的规划成功率提升至100%（LeWM仅97.4%），在OGB-Cube任务中的欧式规划表现比LeWM高14.2分，Push-T任务也有提升。无论用mined代价还是欧式距离，TD-JEPA在所有评测环境下都持平或超过LeWM和RC-aux基线。消融实验显示方向性头、跨轨迹负样本、rollout一致性各自均有积极作用。

Conclusion: TD-JEPA通过从离线轨迹中发现时间推进结构，实现规划阶段与训练阶段的紧密结合，缩小了基于JEPA世界模型的规划在训练与实际部署间的性能差距，取得全面超越现有方法的结果。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn world models by predicting in representation space rather than reconstructing pixels, making them a natural backbone for latent model predictive control from offline demonstration logs. JEPA-style training optimizes short-horizon latent prediction, whereas planning requires a multi-step ranking of imagined futures by goal progress. Prior JEPA planners often inherit that ranking from embedding geometry, typically latent Euclidean distance, which arises as a byproduct of representation learning rather than as a progress cost mined from the logs. We propose temporal-distance JEPA (TD-JEPA), which retains the LeWM encoder--predictor backbone and mines a directed temporal cost from reward-free trajectories: same-trajectory step order supplies positive targets, cross-trajectory pairs act as heuristic negatives, and a rollout-consistency term matches the planner horizon. The mined supervision serves two roles: as the deployed planning cost when progress is topological, and as a representation signal that improves Euclidean planning when contact geometry dominates. Under locked evaluation, deploying the mined cost raises Two-Room success to 100.0% versus LeWM's 97.4%, while shared Euclidean planning on the same temporally trained checkpoint raises OGB-Cube by 14.2 points over LeWM and improves Push-T. Against LeWM and the concurrent RC-aux baseline under locked evaluation, TD-JEPA matches or exceeds both methods on every environment. Ablations show that the directed head, cross-trajectory negatives, and rollout consistency each contribute. TD-JEPA narrows the train--plan gap for JEPA world-model planners by discovering temporal progress structure in offline logs and co-designing cost form with plan-time deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/HKBU-KnowComp/TD-JEPA.

</details>


### [96] [Inspect India Evals: An Open Benchmarking Framework for Evaluating Large Language Models in the Indian Linguistic and Cultural Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25375)
*Abhishek Kumar Singh,Shrey Nag,Sachita,Lipi Goel,Rajeshwar Singh Janwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了针对印度多语言和文化场景的LLM评测框架Inspect India Evals，填补现有欧美英语为中心的基准的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型评测基准（如MMLU、BIG-Bench等）主要关注英语与西方背景，无法覆盖印度独特的语言多样性和文化特性，且无法反映与安全、公平、准确性相关的印度特定挑战。

Method: 提出了Inspect India Evals开源评测框架，包含六个基准：支持16种印度语言的多语言MMLU、关于社会偏见的BharatBBQ、数字公共基础设施的安全评估、多语言有害提示的安全测试、多轮jailbreak测试，以及印度文化知识评测。用5个8B-32B参数量的开源大模型进行了实证评测，并采用LLM作为裁判评分。

Result: Sarvam-M 24B和Gemma 2 27B模型综合表现最好，均获得80%的India Fairness Index分数。Sarvam-M在印度文化知识和DPI安全上甚至超过了更大的32B模型。所有模型在多语言安全测试上拒绝率为100%，但DPI安全得分则差异较大（20%-100%）。

Conclusion: Inspect India Evals提供了系统、可扩展且公开的印度本地化大模型评测方法，有助于今后在印度复杂多元场景下推动LLM安全、公平的部署和优化。

Abstract: India is a vast nation of over 1.4 billion people, varied by hundreds of diverse and locally specific traditions and cultures and 22 officially recognized languages. Large language models (LLMs) are now being deployed on a massive scale throughout the mainland as well as in remote villages. However, the common benchmarks - MMLU, BIG-Bench, and TruthfulQA are almost exclusively English- and Western-centric. They do not identify those safety, fairness, and accuracy failures unique to the Indian context. That is the gap Inspect India Evals seeks to fill. It is an open-source framework built on top of UK AISI's Inspect AI platform. It has six benchmarks: Multilingual MMLU across sixteen Indian languages, BharatBBQ (our adaptation of BBQ for Indian social bias), a safety evaluation for Digital Public Infrastructure, a multilingual safety test using harmful prompts in Indian languages, a multi-turn jailbreak resistance test, and an Indian cultural knowledge benchmark scored using LLM-as-judge rubrics. In this study, we tested five open-weight models ranging from 8B to 32B parameters. Sarvam-M 24B and Gemma 2 27B came out on top, both scoring 80% on the composite India Fairness Index, with Sarvam-M even beating larger 32B models on Indian cultural knowledge and DPI safety compliance. All models scored 100% refusal on Multilingual Safety, whereas DPI safety varied from 20% to 100%. The framework is public. It's built to work with the UK AISI registry. Anyone can reproduce or extend this work.

</details>


### [97] [Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25380)
*Sining Zhoubian,Dan Zhang,Evgeny Kharlamov,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLM）中“记忆”机制的发展与分类，提出了系统化的记忆架构框架，并分析了现有的各种方案与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的进步，记忆机制成为其核心组成部分。由于新近技术的多样性和复杂性，记忆相关研究变得非常零散，因此需要一个统一的分类和分析框架来梳理现状，指导后续研究。

Method: 作者提出以架构为核心的记忆分类法，将记忆按表示方式（隐式/显式）、更新动态（离线/在线）、持久度（短时/长时）三个正交维度进行系统性梳理，并对具体机制（写入、路由、状态转换与巩固）进行了形式化描述和划分。同时，分析了混合记忆架构、效率权衡及多维评估方法。

Result: 本综述构建了统一的记忆机制理论框架，澄清了各类记忆策略间的边界和联系，总结了体系结构、效率及评估等方面的最新研究成果。

Conclusion: 该文章为LLM记忆研究提供了全面基础和清晰方向，有助于未来高效、可扩展、自适应的语言模型设计与发展。

Abstract: Memory has evolved into a foundational architectural dimension in large language models (LLMs), shifting from an implicit byproduct of computation to a spectrum of explicit, controllable mechanisms. While recent advances introduce diverse strategies---spanning transient attention, recurrent state dynamics, parameter-efficient adaptations, and scalable lookup storage---this rapid evolution has led to a highly fragmented research landscape. In this survey, we present a systematic, architecture-centric taxonomy of memory in LLMs. Our framework characterizes memory along three orthogonal axes: representation (implicit versus explicit), update dynamics (offline versus online), and persistence (short-term versus long-term). We further formalize the granular mechanisms dictating memory writing, routing, state transitions, and consolidation. This unified perspective elucidates the conceptual boundaries between computation-coupled and independently addressable memory, effectively bridging disparate architectural paradigms. Additionally, we critically analyze hybrid memory architectures, system-level efficiency trade-offs, and multi-dimensional evaluation methodologies. By consolidating these scattered advancements into a cohesive framework, this survey charts the trajectory of memory-centric LLM design and provides a principled foundation for future innovations in scalable and adaptive language modeling.

</details>


### [98] [Phase Structure in Rotary Attention: A Spectral Framework for Semantic Continuity and Execution-Boundary Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25507)
*Abraham Chachamovits*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的光谱分析框架，用于研究Transformer中旋转位置编码的相位对齐、隐状态连续性及语义漂移，从而丰富对语言模型内部机制的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对Transformer的分析多基于向量几何，容易忽略有序上下文和旋转位置编码所引入的相位结构。本文动机在于通过引入受限的光谱分析方法，更好地理解这部分现象，对当前主流的逐点解释提供理论和方法论支持。

Method: 提出以有序隐状态序列为光谱分解对象，推导了RoPE的attention系数为加权余弦和，证明了相位扰动受限时attention下降受限的稳定性引理。此外，引入了复模态坐标和加权相干泛函，用于超越RoPE原生坐标的相位分析，并明确区分表征连续性与执行边界的判别。

Result: 验证了用所提出的光谱框架，可以准确描述并区分Transformer中内部表征的连续性与最终决策之间的界限。通过分析attention的相位结构与表征连续性，理论上证明了稳定性，并论证某些过渡无法单靠内部连续性判定其合规性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的光谱相位分析方法，为理解Transformer中的旋转编码及其保序性、稳定性和解释边界提供了新工具。拓展了对模型表征连续性和决策边界的机制解释，有助于评估哪些情况下光谱结构主导了内部连续性，又在哪些场景下需要外部规则约束执行。

Abstract: Transformer language models are usually analyzed through vector geometry, yet ordered context and rotary position encoding introduce explicit phase structure into query-key interactions. This paper develops a bounded spectral framework for examining rotary phase alignment, hidden-state continuity, and semantic drift without treating language models as literal physical wave systems. It first identifies ordered hidden-state sequences, rather than vocabulary indices, as valid domains for spectral decomposition. It then derives the Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) attention score as a sum of magnitude-weighted cosine terms and proves a local stability lemma: uniformly bounded phase displacement limits degradation of the corresponding pre-softmax score. To extend phase analysis beyond native RoPE coordinates, the paper defines complex modal coordinates over fixed orthonormal direction pairs and introduces a weighted coherence functional for hidden-state trajectories. These constructions support a strict distinction between representational continuity and execution-boundary admissibility. Internal coherence may describe preservation of task-relevant relations, but it cannot authorize a consequential transition. Positioned against existing geometric, spectral, phase-modulation, representation-analysis, and mechanistic-interpretability accounts, the framework contributes a theoretical and methodological program for determining when spectral structure explains continuity and when governance must remain an external predicate over execution.

</details>


### [99] [IRIS: Reusable Identity Representations from Frozen LLMs for Entity Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25579)
*Xinran Liu,Shengtao Li,Shouqian Shi,Ge Wang,Xin-Wei Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IRIS框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）无训练方式为实体构建独特标识，并实现高效知识图谱实体对齐，在多个基准集上取得出色表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体对齐方法主要依赖图结构和文本信息，难以准确理解异构描述下的实体身份，且基于LLM的方法通常需多次推理，缺乏稳定、可比较的实体表示。

Method: 提出IRIS（Identity Representations from Internal States）框架，冻结LLM，通过实体上下文生成唯一、稳定的身份签名，在共享空间内直接用相似度比对实体，无需针对每对知识图谱或者候选集合重复推理。

Result: 在四个主流实体对齐数据集（D-Y-15K V2、DBP-WIKI、ICEWS-WIKI、ICEWS-YAGO）及两种LLM后端下，最佳IRIS变体的Hits@1分别达到100.00、99.38、98.31和97.99，远超以往方法。

Conclusion: IRIS能够高效、准确地捕获和比较实体身份特征，极大提升了知识图谱实体对齐任务的普适性和性能，同时省去繁琐的模型训练和多次推理开销。

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) identifies entities across knowledge graphs (KGs) that refer to the same real-world object. Conventional EA methods mainly exploit explicit graph structures and textual fields, which often provide insufficient semantic understanding to recognize the same entity under heterogeneous descriptions and distinguish it from semantically similar entities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer deeper entity understanding, existing LLM-based EA methods largely use this capability for auxiliary generation or candidate-conditioned decisions. Consequently, such understanding is not distilled into a stable and directly comparable identity space, leaving alignment tied to specific KG pairs or candidate sets and requiring repeated processing as the matching context changes. To address these limitations, we propose IRIS (Identity Representations from Internal States), a training-free framework that constructs for each entity an iris-like signature encoding its distinctive and stable identity characteristics. IRIS derives these signatures by eliciting identity-oriented contextual representations from a frozen LLM, thereby forming a shared space in which each entity is encoded once and can be aligned across different KGs through direct similarity comparison, without pair-dependent representation construction or candidate-wise LLM inference. Across four established EA benchmarks and two frozen LLM backbones, the best IRIS variants achieve Hits@1 scores of 100.00, 99.38, 98.31, and 97.99 on D-Y-15K V2, DBP-WIKI, ICEWS-WIKI, and ICEWS-YAGO, respectively.

</details>


### [100] [Evaluation of forced alignment of code-mixed speech: the case of Hindi-English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25581)
*Ayushi Pandey,Pamir Gogoi,Kevin Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文评估了蒙特利尔强制对齐工具在印地语-英语混合语音上的表现，提出新词典设计与代码混合数据可显著提高对齐准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混合语音存在新的挑战，如较大的音素库、拼写错误和说话人变化，导致传统单语强制对齐方法难以直接适用。作者希望解决代码混合环境下强制对齐准确性差的问题。

Method: 作者采用蒙特利尔强制对齐器(Montreal Forced Aligner)，针对两大难题：(1) 母语/非母语发音区别导致的自由变体问题和(2) 话语中途英语词的音素边界检测难题。通过引入自举策略优化词典，并训练基于代码混合句层音频的声学模型。

Result: 自举优化后的词典和代码混合数据训练的声学模型，对齐平均误差仅4.15毫秒，比单语(印地语38.18ms、英语37.58ms)误差低十倍。

Conclusion: 设计合理的词典和基于代码混合训练数据的声学模型，对于实现高质量的双语语音强制对齐至关重要。

Abstract: Code-mixed speech poses unique challenges to forced alignment: expanded inventories, orthographic errors, and speaker variation. We evaluate forced alignment of Hindi-English code-mixed speech using the Montreal Forced Aligner. We address 2 problems: (1) free variation involving native vs non-native pairs and (2) phonemic boundary detection for mid-utterance English words. Bootstrapping strategies substantially outperform unmodified lexicons. Acoustic models trained on sentence-level code-mixed data achieve a mean error of 4.15ms, ie. ten times lower than monolingual Hindi (38.18ms) or isolated English (37.58ms) alternatives. Principled lexicon design and code-mixed training data are both essential for reliable alignment of bilingual speech.

</details>


### [101] [PILA: Plug-and-Play Insertion for LLM-native Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25590)
*Zhaowei Zhang,Yuhan Fu,Yihang Zhang,Xiaohan Liu,Ceyao Zhang,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yipeng Kang,Tonghan Wang,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PILA的新方法，将广告插入作为条件性回复重写任务处理，解决了现有广告内容与内容生成耦合导致的兼容性和质量问题。PILA具有高度兼容性且不影响原有模型结构，在提升广告效果的同时保留了回复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）广泛应用，如何自然地在回复中集成广告实现盈利成为关键问题。现有方法将广告与内容生成绑定，导致难以在API或工作流场景下使用，且降低回复质量。

Method: 作者提出PILA，作为独立于主模型的轻量级“sidecar”模块，将广告插入任务转化为回复重写问题，并保持对不同LLM的模型无关性。PILA无需修改主模型或工作流，同时可调节回复自然性和广告曝光度，为实际定价和部署提供灵活接口。

Result: 实验表明PILA在多种主模型上均提升了广告效果，且不损失回复自然性和质量，验证了方法的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: PILA为LLM原生广告提供了实用高效的解决方案，实现了广告效果与内容质量的平衡，适合在现有LLM服务中大规模落地。

Abstract: How to monetize large language models (LLMs) by naturally integrating sponsored content into their responses, known as LLM-native advertising, has recently emerged as a critical problem. However, existing solutions entangle advertising with content generation inside a single model, which is incompatible with modern API-only or workflow-based LLM applications and inevitably compromises the original response quality. To address this, we propose PILA, which reformulates ad insertion as a conditional response rewriting problem and decouples it from the upstream service as a lightweight sidecar module. PILA is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated with existing LLM services without modifying the base model or its workflow. It also exposes a controllable trade-off between user-side naturalness and ad-side exposure, offering a practical interface for downstream pricing and deployment. Experiments across diverse upstream models show that \pila consistently improves ad effectiveness while preserving response quality, highlighting its promise as a practical solution for LLM-native advertising.

</details>


### [102] [A Human-in-the-Loop Corpus for LLM-Based Simplification of Scientific Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25630)
*Kyuri Im,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种结合大语言模型与人工反馈的人机协作科研文本简化流程，产出了可用于跨学科科研交流的简化语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着跨学科研究加速，科研论文对非本领域人员依然难以理解。作者希望通过简化专业术语，提高科学文本的可读性，以促进学科间的交流和知识传播。

Method: 作者基于SciSummNet语料库，先用GPT-4o-mini生成论文摘要简化文本。第一阶段，让非计算机学科的STEM读者标注难懂内容，并评价原文与GPT简化文本的可理解性、自然度、简洁性。第二阶段，由计算机领域专家结合反馈进一步编辑，生成最终专家版参考简化文本。全过程结合人工和自动评价，并公开语料库、人工判分等资源。

Result: 第一阶段，非本领域读者更喜欢GPT生成的简化摘要，认为更易懂且更简洁。第二阶段，专家编辑强调要保留学科术语和科学结论的严谨性。最终成果包括简化语料库及多种评测结果。

Conclusion: 该研究人机结合的简化流程、语料库和评测资源为科研文本跨学科可读性改进以及简化系统的训练与评价提供了重要支撑，有利于推动学科交流和科学普及。

Abstract: Interdisciplinary research is accelerating, yet scientific papers remain difficult to understand outside their home fields. We study large language model (LLM)-based simplification of scientific texts and present a human-in-the-loop workflow that transforms expert summaries into more accessible versions for non-specialists. Using SciSummNet as the source corpus, we first generate baseline simplifications with GPT-4o-mini. In Phase 1, readers from STEM fields outside computer science identify difficult sentences and phrases and compare the original and GPT-simplified summaries in terms of comprehensibility, naturalness, and simplicity. In Phase 2, computer science experts use this feedback to create expert-edited reference simplifications. We release the resulting corpus together with human judgments and automatic evaluation results. The Phase 1 judgments show a clear preference for the GPT-generated summaries in terms of comprehensibility and simplicity, while qualitative analysis of the Phase 2 edits highlights the importance of preserving domain-specific terminology and the strength of scientific claims. The resulting resource supports the training and benchmarking of simplification systems for cross-disciplinary scientific communication.

</details>


### [103] [Construction-Driven Injection: Linguistically-Grounded Edit-Based Code-Mixing Fingerprints for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25633)
*Yongyi Cui,Yue Li,Tianbao Jiang,Xin Yi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合优化指纹构造与注入的新型指纹框架LCF，有效提升了大语言模型所有权验证的可靠性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为高价值的知识资产，易受到未授权分发和商业滥用。现有的模型指纹方案存在自然语言激活偶然性高和乱码指纹易被检测过滤的问题，且指纹构造与注入割裂，导致优化不充分。

Method: 提出LCF框架，包括两部分：第一，通过低资源语言混合和语义密度替换规则构建设计指纹，使其低困惑度且不易意外激活；第二，LCFEdit利用多语种高资源表示生成投影空间，并加入跨语对齐，保证权重更新保持知识和指纹语言语义一致性，实现构造-注入一体化。

Result: 在难以察觉性、检测性和无害性等方面的实验评估显示，该方法能够长期保持所有权验证，对模型实际效用影响极小。

Conclusion: 联动指纹构造与注入可提升大模型指纹的鲁棒性和辨识度，实现低影响下的持续所有权保护，优于现有的分离式指纹框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are costly intellectual assets that remain exposed to unauthorized redistribution and commercial misuse. Injected fingerprints, i.e., trigger--target pairs embedded in model behavior, offer a practical, black-box-verifiable ownership signal, but existing methods decouple the two stages of the fingerprint life cycle: how a fingerprint is constructed and how it is injected. Existing fingerprinting frameworks suffer from two limitations. Natural-language fingerprints are prone to accidental activation, and garbled fingerprints are easily filtered by perplexity-based detection. Furthermore, decoupling construction from injection leaves the latter unaware of the trigger's linguistic structure, missing the opportunity for targeted optimization. We argue that fingerprint construction should drive injection, and present a unified fingerprinting framework that jointly optimizes both stages. First, LCF constructs code-mixing fingerprints by combining low-resource languages under a semantic-density substitution rule and grammar-biased mixing, yielding triggers whose perplexity sits far below garbled baselines while avoiding the accidental-activation failures of natural-language triggers. Second, LCFEdit injects each fingerprint with a null-space projection derived from high-resource multilingual representations that preserves knowledge, augmented by a cross-lingual alignment step that steers the weight update toward the fingerprint language's representation subspace. This construction-aware injection ensures that the update is linguistically informed and therefore more stable. Extensive evaluations on imperceptibility, detectability, and harmlessness demonstrate persistent ownership verification with negligible impact on utility.

</details>


### [104] [MyMentorLLM: A psychotherapy GenAI environment with multimodal voice/text patients, trainees and experts for deliberate practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25667)
*Rodolfo Rizzi,Alessandro Grecucci,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为MyMentorLLM的多模态（语音和文本）模拟训练环境，可用于心理治疗师的认知行为治疗（CBT）训练，提高训练的可扩展性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理治疗师长期需要重复的专家培训与督导，但该过程难以规模化，急需有效的自动化训练工具。

Method: 开发了MyMentorLLM系统，用于通过AI语音和文本生成2100次完整的CBT训练会话，将DSM-5-TR诊断和具有不同心理障碍的病人、实习治疗师和专家督导师模拟集成分析情感动态、治疗能力和诊断准确性。比较了不同LLM和语音模型的表现及反馈对诊断改进的影响。

Result: 模拟病人的情感表现与心理障碍相符，治疗师能进行类似真实咨询的情感回应。不同大模型的督导质量存在差异，原生语音模型较接近真人。督导反馈能提升诊断水平，模型规模越大，症状识别准确性提升。部分模型对治疗师能力高估。

Conclusion: MyMentorLLM验证了通过AI模拟可以提升CBT培训的可能性，但在病人拟真度、督导师校准以及防止误导性反馈三方面仍需进一步完善和评估。

Abstract: Psychotherapists need repeated training and supervision by experts; however, scalability is problematic. Here we present MyMentorLLM, a multimodal voice- and text-based simulation environment for deliberate practice, used to generate 2,100 complete Cognitive Behavioural Therapy (CBT) training sessions. Each session links a DSM-5-TR-grounded patient (with major depressive, generalised anxiety or borderline personality disorder), a therapist-in-training and an expert supervisor. As an initial implementation, we adopted CBT because its structured procedures and competency-based supervision facilitate standardised simulation and evaluation. Sessions were analysed for emotional dynamics, therapeutic competence and diagnostic accuracy. Simulated patients expressed disorder-congruent emotional profiles, which trainee therapists mirrored as in real human counselling. The quality of supervision differed across LLMs: while most models overestimated trainees' competences, native speech-to-speech was closest to human scores. Supervisors' feedback led to better diagnoses in simulated psychotherapists in 5 out of 7 LLMs, and symptom identification accuracy increased with model size. This work shows that simulation of deliberate practice is possible for CBT training, although patient fidelity, calibration of supervisors, and harmful feedback should be evaluated together.

</details>


### [105] [SpeechLLM Meets Federated Learning for End-to-End ASR: English and Italian Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25716)
*Mohamed Nabih Ali,Daniele Falavigna,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统性地研究了大规模语音语言模型（SpeechLLMs）在端到端自动语音识别（ASR）中的联邦学习训练，提出了一种高效的通信优化方法，实现了在保证隐私的同时，沟通成本降低，性能接近中心化训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际场景下，训练语音识别模型的数据分散在多个设备或机构间，难以集中收集且涉及隐私问题。联邦学习可以实现分布式数据的隐私保护训练，但现有针对大模型（尤其是SpeechLLMs）的研究仍有限。该论文旨在解决将联邦学习应用于大规模SpeechLLM模型在ASR上的挑战。

Method: 作者设计了一种面向SpeechLLM架构的、高效通信的联邦优化策略，针对高维参数、梯度通信开销、分布式计算等挑战。在英文和意大利文单语ASR任务上进行了大量实证评估，并通过消融实验对不同语音编码器架构进行分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的联邦学习方法在不同噪声、口音等声学条件下，相较中心化训练表现出良好的稳定性和竞争性的识别准确率（WER），同时有效降低了通信成本。消融实验也揭示了不同编码器架构对模型效果的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为联邦学习在大规模SpeechLLM上的落地提供了实用的技术基础，验证了其在多语种、分布式真实场景下的可行性和有效性，对未来隐私保护的语音识别系统开发具有指导意义。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving training of automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems across distributed data sources, yet its application to large-scale speech language models (SpeechLLMs) remains unexplored. This paper presents the first systematic study of federated training for SpeechLLM-based end-to-end ASR systems. We design a communication-efficient federated optimization strategy tailored to the unique challenges of SpeechLLM architectures, addressing high-dimensional parameter spaces, gradient communication overhead, and computational constraints in distributed settings. Through extensive empirical evaluation on monolingual ASR tasks in English and Italian, we demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of our federated approach compared to centralized training baselines across diverse acoustic conditions and speaking styles. Additionally, we conduct a comprehensive ablation study analyzing the impact of different speech encoder architectures on monolingual English ASR performance within the federated framework, providing insights into optimal model configurations for decentralized training. Our results achieve competitive word error rates while reducing communication costs, establishing practical foundations for federated SpeechLLM deployment in real-world multilingual scenarios.

</details>


### [106] [WorkSurface-Bench: Benchmarking Enterprise Agents on Multi-Surface Knowledge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25765)
*Hao Liang,Meiyi Qiang,Sizhe Qiu,Linzhuang Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的基准 WorkSurface-Bench，用于评测企业级智能体在异构知识源（文档、表格、依赖图）之间精准选择信息表面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流基准通常只评估检索或工具使用能力，忽视了智能体是否能首先正确选择所需的知识源（即表面选择能力），实际企业应用中该能力尤为关键。

Method: 作者构建了包含1,151个原子任务的基准，题目类型覆盖文档、表格、依赖图及跨表面组合题，并为每种题型设计了可审计的标准答案。实验基于四种主流模型主干，在六种受控智能体设置下进行了大规模评测（共27,624条无协议错误的任务路径）。

Result: 实验显示，虽然在限制性工具访问下，模型在表面选择的Route F1高达98.7-99.8，但最终答案准确率仅为56.1-75.3，说明单靠选对信息源还难以实现任务完成。匹配干预实验也揭示，提供表面提示对大多数模型可提升答题表现，而删去无关工具则主要帮助路由和效率。对200个样本任务的三人独立质检显示所有任务均通过全部六项质量标准。

Conclusion: WorkSurface-Bench适合作为衡量智能体多源信息选择能力的基准，可促进具备更强现实应用适应性的企业智能体系统研发。数据集及评测工具已开源，便于后续研究与社区迭代。

Abstract: Enterprise agents often need to integrate heterogeneous knowledge sources: documents for narrative facts, tables for computation, and dependency graphs for file relationships. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate retrieval or tool use without distinguishing whether an agent first selects the appropriate knowledge sources. We introduce WorkSurface-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating this capability as surface routing. It contains 1,151 atomic tasks derived from persona-scoped Workspace-Bench-Lite workspaces, spanning document, table, graph, and cross-surface questions. Its reference answers are auditable: table answers are reproduced through executed DuckDB queries, document answers are grounded in verified text spans, and graph answers are traced to source dependency annotations. We evaluate four model backbones across six controlled agent settings, yielding 27,624 protocol-error-free trajectories. Under gold-constrained tool access, agents achieve 98.7-99.8 Route F1, while Answer remains only 56.1-75.3 percent, showing that correct surface selection is necessary but insufficient for task completion. Matched interventions further show that surface hints improve Answer for three of four models, whereas removing irrelevant tools primarily improves routing and efficiency. In an independent three-annotator audit, all 200 sampled tasks pass all six quality criteria by majority vote, with 192 receiving unanimous judgments on every criterion. We release the dataset, construction pipeline, scoring code, and agent harness at https://github.com/haolpku/WorkSurface-Bench.

</details>


### [107] [Evaluation of Adversarial Robustness in Arabic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25814)
*Anwar Alajmi,Ayed Salman,Imtiaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了五个最新阿拉伯语大模型在多种细粒度对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，并探讨了对抗训练提升防御能力的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代阿拉伯语大模型在NLP表现突出，但安全性和鲁棒性问题尚未充分研究，尤其是面对对抗攻击。该研究旨在揭示这些模型在多层次、多策略攻击下的脆弱性，并寻找提升鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 作者设计了相应的阿拉伯语字符级、词级、句子级对抗攻击手法，包括添加重音符号、操纵连词与意译变换，通过自动/手动生成多样攻击样本。并对五个主流阿拉伯语模型进行鲁棒性评估，同时采用对抗训练作为防御措施进行对比分析。

Result: 结果显示，字符级添加重音可令部分模型准确率下降92%，词级连词操纵使准确率最高下跌58%，句子级意译平均降低性能76%。对抗训练提升了所有模型在部分攻击下的鲁棒性，MARBERT表现最稳健，AraBERT相对提升最大，但字符级噪声依然难以防御。

Conclusion: 当前防御策略对形态复杂语言如阿拉伯语仅能提供有限保护。对抗训练虽有改进作用，但提升鲁棒性的难题尚存，未来需进一步加强多层次鲁棒防御研究。

Abstract: The emergence of the recent outstanding capabilities of Arabic Language Models has opened doors for exposing their vulnerabilities. One of the major security risks associated with such Natural Language Processing models is adversarial attacks. These attacks can deceive the model into the wrong prediction, raising critical model security and safety concerns. This study aims to assess the robustness of five state-of-the-art Arabic Language Models under a distinct set of Arabic adversarial attacks applied at various levels of granularity and using different example generation strategies. We also explore a defense technique based on adversarial training to enhance model robustness. The results show that insertion of diacritics can reduce the accuracy of some models by 92% while maintaining a low perturbation distance. For word-level attacks, manipulating Arabic conjunctions preserves high semantic similarity scores, low perturbation distance, and leads to an accuracy degradation of up to 58%. For sentence-level attacks, paraphrasing proves its effectiveness by an average reduction of 76% in the victim models' performance. While adversarial training improves overall resilience, with MARBERT being the most robust and AraBERT showing the greatest relative gains, challenges persist, particularly against character-level noise. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of current defense strategies in morphologically rich languages like Arabic.

</details>


### [108] [AngelSpec: Towards Real-World High Performance Inference with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25852)
*Hong Liu,Rui Cen,Junhan Shi,Guangshuo Qin,Jiebin Zhang,Tianyu Liu,Runzhi Fan,Guoliang Zhao,Ruobing Xie,Kai Zhang,Song Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AngelSpec提出了一个统一的训练框架，结合MTP与区块并行推理，实现推理加速并适应不同应用场景。DFly新结构显著提升了LLM的推理长度与吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理加速方法如speculative decoding虽不改变分布，但不同草稿结构性能各异，缺乏可针对多场景高效适配的训练与架构。

Method: 1. 训练层面：针对多样数据（对话/代码/数学）分别训练MTP和区块扩散模型实现协同优化。
2. 架构层面：提出DFly，结合目标条件主干与自回归头部，有效利用目标特征并建模区块内依赖，实现并行生成。
3. 推理层面：批量共享校验计算资源，结合效用与成本模型，动态自适应调整校验深度与算力分配。

Result: 在Hy3-A21B模型上平均接受长度提升约30%；在4到64并发场景下始终获得最高吞吐率，相较普通自回归解码提速约2倍，相比DFlash吞吐量提高约11%。

Conclusion: AngelSpec与DFly方法有效应对不同类型任务负载，提升推理效率和吞吐表现，为后续方法研究与实践提供了可用框架与工具。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference without changing the target distribution, but no single drafting structure performs best across real-world workloads. Autoregressive multi-token prediction (MTP) is a lightweight, stable proposal mechanism, whereas block-parallel diffusion amortizes drafting latency over much longer candidate sequences; the better choice depends strongly on the output distribution.
