We present AngelSpec, a unified training framework for MTP and block-parallel speculative decoding that addresses this heterogeneity at three levels. At the training level, rather than fitting one universal drafter to a uniform data mixture, we co-specialize structure and data: the MTP drafter is trained on diverse conversational data for high-entropy open-ended chat, and the block-diffusion drafter on code and mathematics data for longer predictable continuations. At the architecture level, we propose DFly, a block-diffusion framework combining a hybrid target-conditioning backbone with a predecessor-conditioned autoregressive head, improving target-feature utilization and intra-block dependency modeling while keeping generation parallel. At the inference level, both acceptance length and verification cost vary with domain, request, online load, and hardware, so DFly treats verification as a shared batch-level resource: it reallocates compute toward high-confidence prefixes across requests and combines expected utility with a profiled cost model to adapt verification depth online. Across the Hy3 series, DFly raises the average accepted length on Hy3-A21B by roughly 30% and attains the highest average throughput at every tested concurrency from 4 to 64, a 1.98-2.40x speedup over autoregressive decoding and 10.5-11.8% higher throughput than DFlash. We release AngelSpec to support training and extending these methods.

</details>


### [109] [VisualPatchWorld: Code World Models as Latent Structured Representations for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25236)
*Jiaxin Bai,Jiaxuan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的世界模型方法VisualPatchWorld（VPW），用代码形式表示和拟合世界动态，结合了神经网络和物理引擎的优点，并在规划任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型方法主要有两类：神经网络动态模型和手工物理引擎。前者善于从大量数据中学习但缺乏可解释性；后者结构明晰可编辑但难以大规模构建。需要有自动、可解释、易于扩展且能支持感知与规划的世界模型。

Method: VPW首先通过短暂主动探测选择合适的动态模型类型，然后根据历史状态-动作轨迹拟合模型参数（最小化多步预测误差），最终以程序代码的形式表达动态模型，支持推理、可读源码，以及在模型预测控制中的应用。同时可由图像获取的场景图提供实时状态。

Result: 与以往代码世界模型相比，VPW在平均规划成功率达69.0%，超越最强基线23.5个百分点。若准确选择动态类型，提升最明显。与真实物理引擎对照，VPW在导航和抓取等任务接近真实表现，推拽物体任务有一定差距，但采用短列表的重检查策略能进一步缩小差距。

Conclusion: VPW为自动化构建可读、可编辑且实用的代码化世界模型提供了一条有效途径，能显著提升规划任务的效果。

Abstract: Different research lines use the term world model in different ways, yet they share a common aim: to capture how the world evolves under action in a form that supports perception, simulation, and planning. Two prominent realizations are neural predictors that learn dynamics in continuous vector spaces, and hand-built physics engines that expose explicit state and physical laws. Neural predictors scale from data but leave the form of the dynamics implicit; physics engines are inspectable and editable but difficult to construct at scale. We introduce VisualPatchWorld (VPW), which represents world dynamics as code. VPW first selects a qualitative dynamical form with short active probes, then fits that form's free parameters from recorded state-action traces by minimizing multi-step prediction error. The resulting programs can be rolled forward like a simulator, inspected in source form, and used inside model-predictive control; image-derived scene graphs can supply the live state at replan time. Across comparisons with prior code-based world models, VPW attains 69.0% mean planning success and exceeds the strongest code baseline by 23.5 points. The largest gains arise when choosing the correct qualitative dynamics is essential. Under the same planner, the induced models approach ground-truth engine success on navigation and grasp-rich control; a residual gap remains for contact-rich pushing, and checking a shortlist of promising plans in the engine closes most of that gap. These results establish a practical route toward automatically constructed code world models that are useful for planning. Code is available at https://github.com/HKBU-KnowComp/VisualPatchWorld/.

</details>


### [110] [Shieldstral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25857)
*Antonia Calvi,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Giada Pistilli,Guillaume Lample,Maarten Buyl,Maximilian Augustin,Maximilian Müller,Pierre Stock,Tom Bewley,Wassim Bouaziz,Yimu Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Shieldstral是一种3B参数的多模态安全分类器，性能媲美甚至超过体积大约7倍的同类模型，在多模态安全分类领域创下新纪录。该模型将内容审核转化为二元问答任务，统一多样化的审核任务为简单的“是/否”问题。通过对约5410万样本的数据进行构建和筛选，Shieldstral展示出较强的适应性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态内容安全审核任务中，不同安全数据集标签体系存在差异，使得训练大一统的模型变得困难。大模型虽表现优异，但资源消耗较高，亟需一种小模型兼容多样审核任务，并有效适应不同政策要求的方法。

Method: Shieldstral将内容审核问题转化为二元问答（是/否）任务，以此统一不同类型和标签体系的数据集。在数据构建方面，作者整理和生成了约5410万条样本，并开发了细粒度的评测集来验证模型对不同政策的适应能力。

Result: Shieldstral在文本安全基准测试中表现出色，达到或超越了体积大约7倍的模型，同时在多模态安全分类任务上创下了新的结果。尤其在数据异质性和政策适应性评测上，展示了小模型的强大竞争力。

Conclusion: 此项工作表明，通过统一的问题设定和高质量大规模多样数据集，小型的多模态安全分类模型不仅能够适应多变的政策，还能达到甚至超越大型模型在内容审核领域的性能。

Abstract: We introduce Shieldstral, a 3B-parameter policy-adaptive multimodal safety classifier that matches or outperforms models nearly 7$\times$ its size on text safety benchmarks and sets a new state of the art on multimodal safety classification. Shieldstral formulates content moderation as a binary question-answering task. This simple formulation unifies diverse moderation tasks into a single yes/no problem, enabling heterogeneous safety datasets with divergent taxonomies to be consolidated under one training framework. We present the data construction recipe, covering curation and generation of approximately 54.1M samples and a fine-grained evaluation set to evaluate policy adaptability. Together, these enable a small adaptive model to match or outperform much larger models.

</details>


### [111] [AI's Capability in Assisting Scientific Research in Physics, Astrophysics, and Cosmology II: Project Planning and Proposal Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25881)
*Jia Liu,Veena Krishnaraj,Kateryna Vovk,Kosuke Aizawa,Adrian E. Bayer,Linda Blot,Jessica Cowell,Suyog Garg,Jonathan Grée,Anamaria Hell,Ben Horowitz,Masaya Ichikawa,Kanyuni Iemoto,Keigo Kondo,Zacharie Lorsin,Kevin McCarthy,Jamie Robinson,Miguel Ruiz-Granda,Leander Thiele,Ievgen Vovk,Mingshen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了人类与大语言模型（LLM）撰写的科学项目计划书在人类与AI审稿人评估下的表现，发现LLM撰写的计划书在人类评审中表现与人工相当，但AI评审更偏好AI所写内容，且AI分辨人/AI所写计划书准确率远高于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力提升，越来越多科研人员考虑用其协助科学项目策划与提案，但实际LLM撰写项目计划书的质量、以及LLM参与评审的影响尚不明朗，因此论文欲探究LLM在项目书写作和评审中的表现及潜在偏差。

Method: 由专家设想8个物理、天体物理、宇宙学项目，邀请人类研究者与三款主流LLM（ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek）分别独立生成项目计划，一共32份。再用4位人类审稿人与2款更新的大模型AI审稿人（Claude Opus 4.8及ChatGPT Pro 5.5）对这些计划书进行盲评，依据四项标准打分，并尝试判断作者是人或AI。

Result: 人类审稿人对人类和AI撰写的方案给分相当，而AI审稿人对AI撰写的方案比人类撰写高1分左右（满分5分）。人类能较好分辨作者身份（准确率72%-79%），AI审稿人则分类准确率100%。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型产生的科研项目计划书对人类评审者而言已接近人工水平，但AI评审对AI写作有系统性偏好，提醒在科研提案的撰写与审核流程中需要警惕AI引入的偏见，慎重应用相关技术。

Abstract: We investigate how well large language models (LLMs) can assist scientific project planning and proposal evaluation. One-page project plans were independently generated for eight expert-conceived research projects in physics, astrophysics, and cosmology by human researchers and three contemporary LLMs (ChatGPT, Claude, and DeepSeek; mid-2025 models, used with their default tool access). The resulting 32 proposals were blindly evaluated by four human reviewers and two newer frontier LLMs (Claude Opus 4.8 and ChatGPT Pro 5.5) using a four-aspect evaluation rubric. Reviewers were also asked to identify whether each proposal was written by a human or an AI. Human reviewers rated human- and AI-written proposals similarly overall, whereas both AI reviewers scored AI-written proposals about one point higher (on a five-point scale) than human-written proposals. Human reviewers correctly identified human- and AI-written proposals 72% and 79% of the time, respectively, while both AI reviewers correctly classified all 32 proposals (100%). These results suggest that current LLMs can produce project plans comparable to human-written ones in the eyes of human reviewers, but that AI reviewers show a systematic preference for AI-generated proposals. Our results suggest caution when deploying LLMs widely in proposal preparation and evaluation.

</details>


### [112] [Evaluating Multi-Turn Multimodal Diagnostic Reasoning on Challenging Real-World Clinical Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25933)
*Rui Yang,Weihao Xuan,Yi Lin,Zhuhan Bao,Jonathan Chong Kai Liew,Matthew Yu Heng Wong,Nicolás Lescano,Nikita R. Paripati,Emily Ling-Lin Pai,Jiarui Liu,Heli Qi,Heng-Jui Chang,Benny Kai Guo Loo,Huitao Li,Kunyu Yu,Yufan Wang,Chuan Hong,Shijian Lu,Douglas Teodoro,Naoto Yokoya,Ross Koppel,Mona Diab,Hua Xu,David W. Bates,Nan Liu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布了ClinMM-Bench，这是至今最大规模的多轮多模态临床诊断评测基准，用于更全面地评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在临床诊断中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对MLLMs的临床诊断评测多为单轮或孤立任务，无法反映现实临床实践的复杂性和诊断过程的动态性。本研究旨在填补该评测空白。

Method: 构建ClinMM-Bench数据集，包含1,089个真实复杂病例案例和3,760个医学图像，覆盖八大专科；并提出两级评测体系，系统性评估15个代表性MLLMs的诊断准确性和推理质量，辅以错误分析。

Result: 部分私有模型整体诊断准确率最高，但所有模型完全正确诊断比例都较低。在推理质量上，各模型能合理指出可疑诊断方向，但存在显著不足。错误分析总结了五种典型失效模式。

Conclusion: 现有MLLMs在临床诊断准确性和推理可靠性方面均有明显不足。ClinMM-Bench能有效揭示和推动未来医疗AI模型的改进方向。

Abstract: Clinical diagnostic evaluation should not only assess whether models can provide correct diagnoses, but also reflect the realities of clinical practice, including progressive disclosure of multimodal information, dynamic updating of diagnostic hypotheses, and continuous refinement of clinical reasoning. However, existing evaluations of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically rely on single-turn or isolated tasks, making it difficult to fully capture the complexity of real-world clinical diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we developed ClinMM-Bench, the largest multi-turn multimodal clinical diagnostic evaluation benchmark to date. ClinMM-Bench contains 1,089 challenging real-world clinical cases and 3,760 medical images across eight specialties. We systematically evaluated 15 representative MLLMs using a two-level evaluation framework that assessed both diagnostic accuracy and diagnostic reasoning quality. Results showed that proprietary models achieved the highest overall diagnostic accuracy, but the proportion of completely correct diagnoses remained limited across all models. In terms of diagnostic reasoning quality, current models can identify plausible diagnostic directions but still have considerable limitations in generating reliable diagnostic reasoning. Error analysis further identified five representative failure modes: information synthesis failure, knowledge mapping error, perception error, premature closure, and visual hallucination.

</details>


### [113] [Polistemics: Evaluating LLMs as Information Mediators in Politics & Elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25953)
*Baran Peters*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Polistemics基准，用于系统评估大模型（LLMs）在选举政治信息中作为中介者的表现，并通过2025年德国和荷兰选举具体实测，发现现有模型可靠性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在公民获取政治信息中作用增强，目前还没有标准方法评估其信息中介的责任性。此前工作多关注信息重现，忽视了中介过程的知识论维度和不完善信息下的相互作用，这促使作者提出新的测评框架。

Method: 作者基于‘知识谦逊’理论（Epistemic Modesty）建立评测标准，在多个控制情境下（如信息清晰度、噪声、矛盾性等）测试三种主流LLM在德国和荷兰选举中对政治信息的中介表现。

Result: LLMs在证据清晰时表现良好，但在信息缺失、模糊或矛盾时，容易出现系统性失败，并且对党派标签和输出语言有先验偏好，导致政治语言表达强度被削弱。整体分数掩盖了潜在的不一致与缺陷。

Conclusion: 高可靠性的信息中介是可以实现的，但当前没有任何模型能始终如一地做到这一点，存在明显改进空间。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly mediate the political information citizens rely on, there is still no standardized way to assess whether they do so responsibly. We introduce Polistemics, a theory-grounded benchmark for evaluating LLMs as mediators of political information in elections. Prior work has treated this task as reproduction rather than mediation, leaving its epistemic dimensions and interaction with imperfect information unaddressed. We ground the evaluation in Epistemic Modesty, a normative standard derived from citizens' epistemic agency, and test it across controlled settings that vary informational properties such as clarity, noise, and consistency. Applying the benchmark to three state-of-the-art LLMs on the 2025 German and Dutch elections, we find that high aggregate scores mask systematic failures. Models mediate reliably under clear evidence but break down under absent, vague, or contradictory information, while flattening the intensity of political language. These failures are likely driven by party priors, influenced by party labels and output language. Reliable mediation appears achievable, but no model delivers it consistently.

</details>


### [114] [Detecting Knowledge Inconsistencies Across Text, Tables, and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25959)
*Fanfu Wei,Thibault Ehrhart,Raphaël Troncy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kontrast框架，用于检测和解释维基百科文本、表格和知识图谱之间的多模态不一致性问题，并验证该方法在大规模知识审计中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维基百科和Wikidata等多模态知识库广泛应用于信息获取和大语言模型训练，但其知识分布于文本、表格和知识图谱，容易出现内容冲突。现有方法缺乏自动化检测和解释这些跨模态不一致的方法，因此需要工具来提升知识质量与一致性。

Method: 作者提出了跨模态不一致性的分类体系，涵盖信息粒度差异、直接冲突、时间变化和知识图谱不完整等类型。进一步开发了Kontrast自动化框架，利用Text-to-SPARQL转换和LLM推理，自动比对表格答案与知识图谱证据，分类并分析不一致性。

Result: 实验表明，多模态之间的不一致性普遍存在，能够揭示真实知识冲突、缺失的知识结构和时间错配，但也容易受到Text-to-SPARQL误差和噪声的影响。

Conclusion: 文本、表格和知识图谱可通过系统比对互补和纠错。Kontrast为大规模知识审计提供实用工具，也为后续研究建立了跨模态一致性基准。

Abstract: Wikipedia and Wikidata are widely used for information access, LLM pre-training, and retrieval-augmented generation. Their knowledge is deeply connected but scattered across text, tables, and knowledge graphs. This raises a practical question: when these modalities disagree, how can we detect and explain the conflict? We study this problem as \emph{modality-level inconsistency detection}. We first introduce a taxonomy of cross-modal knowledge inconsistencies, covering information granularity differences, direct conflicts, temporal changes, and KG incompleteness. We then present \textsc{Kontrast}, an automatic framework that uses Text-to-SPARQL and LLM reasoning to compare table-based answers with KG evidence and categorize the resulting inconsistencies. Experiments on various Table-QA datasets show that cross-modal inconsistencies are common and informative. They reveal not only true knowledge conflicts, but also missing KG structure and temporal mismatches while being limited by Text-to-SPARQL errors and noise. Our analysis shows that text, tables, and KGs can complement and correct one another through systematic comparison. \textsc{Kontrast} provides a practical tool for large-scale knowledge auditing and establishes a benchmark for future work on cross-modal knowledge consistency. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ECLADATTA/KONTRAST.

</details>


### [115] [Instruction-Tuned Models Locally Reuse Human Syntax More Than Humans Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26015)
*Zandi Eberstadt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（如Llama和Gemma）在句法趋同方面是否与人类类似，结果表明模型在多种句法结构上有明显的趋同特性，尤其是在低频规则上更突出。指令微调后的模型与人类或预训练版本在句法、语义和词汇相似性上表现不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句法趋同是人类对话中的普遍现象，但尚不清楚大模型在与人类互动中是否展现出类人句法趋同，尤其是针对各种句法结构。理解这一点有助于评估大模型对话的自然性和人类相似性。

Method: 采用替代范式，让LLM生成内容替代真实对话中一方，测量LLM与前一句人类回复在CFG句法规则上的重叠，并与无关样本对比。分析了16种Llama和Gemma模型（1B-70B，预训练与指令微调版）的1901组匹配对话。同时还在词汇和语义相似性上做了比较分析。

Result: 所有模型与上一个人类轮次的CFG规则重叠高于随机对比，且在低频规则上更明显。指令微调模型在实际语料上的重叠高于替换掉的人类回复。指令微调后，模型对无关样本的重叠增大，实际与随机间的提升减小，条件句法规则重用概率变低。模型在词汇和语义上与前一句更为相似，指令微调后语义相似性大幅提升。

Conclusion: 大模型确实表现出类似人类的句法趋同，但在指令微调后表现更加明显且更偏向语义趋同。预训练与指令微调模型间存在显著句法和语义差异，提示微调方式影响模型在对话中的“人类化”表现。

Abstract: Syntactic convergence (the tendency of speakers to adapt in language towards the grammatical profiles of their interlocutors) is a well-documented feature of human dialogue widely considered to operate below conscious awareness. Whether large language models exhibit analogous syntactic convergence toward human users relative to human baselines and across a broad range of syntactic constructions remains an open question. Using substitution-paradigm data in which model generations replace one speaker's turns in pre-existing human dialogues, this study measures turn-adjacent reuse of context-free grammar (CFG) rules across sixteen open-weight Llama and Gemma models (1B-70B, pretrained and instruction-tuned) at 1,901 matched positions per model. Every model showed greater CFG-rule overlap with the preceding human turn than with a sampled unrelated human prime, and in every model this actual-versus-random difference was larger for lower-frequency rules. Each instruction-tuned model also showed greater natural-output overlap with the actual prime than the human response it replaced, and all eight matched architecture pairs exhibited greater actual-prime overlap after instruction tuning. However, relative to pretrained variants, instruction-tuned outputs overlapped more with unrelated primes, showed a smaller actual-versus-random increment, and had lower conditional rule-reuse odds once target rule-set size was held constant. In exploratory analyses, each model exhibited greater mean lexical and semantic similarity to the preceding turn than the matched human responses did. Instruction-tuned models additionally produced responses with greater mean semantic similarity than their pretrained counterparts in all eight architecture pairs, whereas the lexical similarity results were more heterogeneous.

</details>


### [116] [UniMem: Complementary Episodic-to-Parametric Memory for Boundary-Agnostic Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26017)
*Siyu Xia,Chenheng Zhang,Yanting Wu,Haoxuan Li,Jiajun Chai,Xiaohan Wang,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhouchen Lin,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应记忆管理框架UniMem，通过自路由机制实现大模型任务记忆的高效扩展与转移，提升了多任务处理中的稳定性和记忆效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型（LLM）在处理连续、多变任务流时，传统的记忆方案（如外部检索型或参数型记忆）在稳定与可塑性之间存在权衡：外部记忆虽然能快速适应新任务，却难以巩固策略且推理开销大；参数型记忆需依赖明确的任务边界且扩展受限，难以灵活应对实际应用需求。

Method: 受人脑记忆机制启发，作者提出UniMem，通过可学习路由token作为记忆管理器，动态协调“情节记忆”（episodic buffer）和“参数记忆”（parametric memory）的互补作用：新颖或稀疏任务用情节记忆进行检索增强，重复稳定模式则被巩固进可扩展参数记忆。UniMem利用路由token和参数记忆块分离任务识别与执行，能在无任务标签和可控参数增长的情况下按需扩展记忆能力。

Result: 在长时序、多任务流场景的实验中，UniMem在三种主干大模型上均优于现有基线，平均提升4.0个EM点，并保持了执行的准确性。

Conclusion: UniMem能够有效兼顾记忆的稳定性与可塑性，实现高效自适应的任务经验积累与迁移，为大模型智能体的长周期应用提供了新方法。

Abstract: Memory is essential for LLM agents to accumulate task experience and reuse task-specific execution strategies. However, real-world deployment over boundary-agnostic and evolving task streams exposes a fundamental stability-plasticity dilemma. External retrieval-based memory can rapidly absorb new evidence, but it often fails to internalize recurring execution patterns and incurs inference-time retrieval overhead. Parametric memory enables stable and efficient execution once learned, but typically relies on explicit task boundaries and fixed parameter budgets. Inspired by the human brain, which balances plasticity and stability through complementary episodic storage and gradual consolidation, we propose UniMem, a self-routing framework for autonomous memory management. UniMem uses learnable routing tokens as memory controllers, enabling adaptive coordination between complementary memory pathways: novel or sparse tasks are retained in an episodic buffer for retrieval-augmented execution, while recurring and reliable patterns are consolidated into expandable parametric memory. By decoupling task identification from task execution with routing tokens and parametric memory blocks, UniMem expands memory on demand without task labels during deployment or uncontrolled parameter growth. Experiments on long-horizon streaming task sequences show that UniMem consistently outperforms baselines while maintaining execution fidelity, achieving an average gain of 4.0 EM points across three backbone models.

</details>


### [117] [Pass the Baton: Trajectory-Relayed On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26057)
*Haolei Xu,Xiaowen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Zixuan Ni,Hongxing Li,Yiwen Qiu,Weiming Lu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Relay-OPD方法，有效解决了传统on-policy distillation（OPD）中的前缀错误累积问题，通过让教师在关键点接管生成一段内容后再交回给学生，显著提升了学生模型在数学推理任务上的表现，同时降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OPD在学生模型生成错误推理方向（前缀失败）时，会导致后续生成都基于这个错误方向，难以纠正，并且浪费监督和计算资源。作者希望解决学生模型在错误轨迹上难以回撤修正的问题。

Method: 提出Relay-OPD：训练中动态检测到学生生成出现可能走向错误推理的前缀后，立即让教师模型接管生成，随后再交还给学生继续生成，学生再依据‘接力’后的完整轨迹进行优化。整个过程限定了教师介入（relay）的次数，以保持学生主体和限制偏离。

Result: 在8个数学推理评测基准上，1.7B参数的学生模型使用Relay-OPD平均超越标准OPD 5.73%、超越FastOPD 1.49%；0.6B模型同样持续受益。同时训练所需轨迹长度缩短超过50%。

Conclusion: Relay-OPD有效克服了OPD中前缀失败导致的偏差累积，提升了模型性能和训练效率，为指令微调和推理任务中的知识蒸馏提供了更优解法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) grounds token-level supervision in the student's own trajectory, yet suffers from prefix failure: once the student commits to a wrong reasoning direction, all subsequent generation builds on this deviation, producing misdirected continuations that elicit unreliable supervision and waste compute. We identify a teacher-student continuation asymmetry on failed prefixes, where the teacher tends to redirect while the student continues along the original direction, and convert it into a label-free handoff trigger in Relay On-Policy Distillation (Relay-OPD). During training, Relay-OPD constructs relay trajectories by letting the teacher briefly take over at detected trigger points to produce a teacher leg, after which the student resumes and is optimized on the resulting trajectory. A limited relay budget concentrates intervention on critical early positions while limiting departure from the student policy. With a Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 teacher and Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B-Non-Thinking students on eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, Relay-OPD achieves the best or second-best results on every benchmark, outperforming standard OPD by +5.73% and the strongest baseline FastOPD by +1.49% on average for 1.7B, with consistent gains at 0.6B. Training trajectory length is reduced by over 50%.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Egocentric Station Holding of Robotic Fish in Unknown Turbulent Background Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24860)
*Xiaozhu Lin,Xu Huang,Hongru Dai,Xiaopei Liu,Junzhi Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SWiFT框架，结合仿真与实验平台，利用强化学习显著提升机器人鱼在湍流环境中定点保持能力，且无须额外流速传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人鱼若要在自然水域实际应用，必需具备在流动水域中接近目标并稳定驻留的能力。现有研究尽管提升了游动效率及机动性，但对流体-结构强非线性耦合缺乏深入表征，导致上述能力发展滞后。

Method: 提出SWiFT框架：结合机器人鱼自由游动实验平台、高效物理一致的CFD仿真器，并构建系统性的仿真到现实迁移流程，通过强化学习探索本体/尾鳍驱动鱼体的站定策略。

Result: SwiFT获得的策略在包括距离均方根误差等全部指标上，相较现有方法取得显著提升。实验还表明，仅凭自身运动反馈即可实现高效定点，无需外部流速感知，类似生物鱼顺流定向(rheotaxis)现象。

Conclusion: SWiFT显著推进了机器人鱼实用化控制能力，为未来水下机器人复杂游动任务的解决提供了有力工具和理论基础。

Abstract: Approaching a target position and holding station in flowing water is a fundamental and critical capability for robotic fish operating in natural aquatic environments. Despite decades of advances in enhancing swimming efficiency and maneuverability, this capability remains underdeveloped, largely owing to the insufficiently characterized, highly nonlinear fluid-structure interactions inherent to freely swimming robotic fish in flows. To bridge this gap, we propose the SWiFT framework, a Swimming With Flow Toolbox that enables the efficient exploration of an egocentric station-holding policy for a body and/or caudal fin (BCF) robotic fish in unknown and turbulent background flows via reinforcement learning (RL). Our SWiFT integrates a free-swimming flow-tank experimental setup with a highly efficient, physically consistent computational fluid dynamics (CFD)-based simulator and a systematic sim-to-real transfer pipeline. The resulting policy achieves substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods across all metrics, most notably root-mean-square error (RMSE) of distance. Furthermore, we validated that egocentric feedback alone, without any explicit flow sensing, enables station-holding in unknown turbulent flows, closely mirroring the biological phenomenon of rheotaxis. Accordingly, the success of this egocentric station-holding policy not only advances robotic fish control toward real-world deployment, but also highlights SWiFT's promise as a foundation for tackling complex swimming tasks for underwater robots.

</details>


### [119] [Steeringless Drifting: Differential-Torque Control of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24863)
*Sheng Zhao,Zexin Wu,Dongyang Zhou,Bolin Zhao,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无机械转向的四轮独立驱动(4WID)车辆的差速漂移控制方法。利用差动力矩实现直接横摆力矩，从而实现漂移。通过仿真和微缩实车实验验证了方法的有效性和可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶和新型底盘架构的发展，传统的漂移控制方法（依赖机械转向和后轮饱和）已不适用于无机械转向的4WID车辆。该类架构如何实现极限操控，尤其是漂移控制，成为新的技术挑战。

Method: 建立了含四轮差动执行的双轨车辆模型，并基于该模型提出了漂移平衡解算方法以及闭环漂移控制器，利用轮端差动力矩实现车辆漂移操控。

Result: 通过1:10的微缩车辆仿真和实验，车辆能够实现大约20度侧滑角的稳定圆形漂移，并能够完成八字形漂移轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 验证了仅依靠差动力矩即可实现无转向车辆的漂移控制，展示了新型无转向极限控制的可行性，并为相关车辆底盘控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Control methods for emerging vehicle chassis architectures are important for autonomous driving near handling limits. Unlike conventional drift control, which relies on mechanical steering and rear-tire saturation, a steering-free four-wheel independently driven (4WID) vehicle can generate direct yaw moment through differential wheel torques. This paper proposes a differential-torque drift control method for such a vehicle. A double-track vehicle model incorporating four-wheel differential actuation is established, based on which a drift-equilibrium calculation method and a closed-loop drift controller are developed. The proposed approach is validated through simulations and experiments on a 1:10-scale vehicle. The results show that the vehicle can achieve steady circular drifting with a sideslip angle of approximately 20$^\circ$ and perform figure-eight drift tracking. This study demonstrates the feasibility of drift control using only differential wheel torques and provides a new perspective on near-limit control for steering-free vehicle architectures.

</details>


### [120] [Amortising Trajectory Optimisation for Residual MPC via Implicit Contact Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24959)
*Daniel Layeghi,Thomas Corbères,Calum Arnott,Aditya Kamireddypalli,Hashim Al-Obaidi,Steve Tonneau,Michael Mistry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于隐式函数定理（IFT）的可微仿真方法，大幅降低了存储成本，并提升了接触丰富任务中的轨迹优化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微仿真方法要么计算量大且对步长敏感（有限差分），要么自动微分时内存消耗迅速增加（反复展开求解器），要么需手动推导复杂的灵敏度公式（KKT体系）。因此需要一种存储成本低、适用范围广且能直接应用于标准仿真器的新方法。

Method: 作者将自动微分辅助的隐式导数方法引入平滑正则化接触模型，并应用于Mujoco MJX模拟器。他们对求解器收敛后的驻留残差做导数处理，规避了求解器展开和手动KKT灵敏度分析。此外，引入优化器蒸馏，将全时域iLQR的优化知识迁移到用于剩余MPC的策略中。

Result: 新方法下，IFT方案的内存从一次到十次迭代增长不到4%，而标准展开AD内存增长高达10.6倍。在大规模接触或高自由度模型下，IFT内存消耗明显低于基线（最多低20倍）。在Finger、Franka和Unitree等任务上，六步任务成功率较标准iLQR提升了28至98个百分点。

Conclusion: IFT可微分仿真显著降低了优化所需的存储资源，对高接触数和高自由度问题适用性强。优化器蒸馏进一步提升了控制任务表现，推动了轨迹优化方法在机器人等领域的实用性。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation can accelerate contact-rich trajectory optimisation by exposing local sensitivities of task outcomes to controls. Existing approaches either use finite differences, which are expensive and step-size sensitive; differentiate iterative contact solvers by unrolling automatic differentiation (AD), which stores a growing computation trace; or require intricate, solver-specific KKT sensitivity derivations. We introduce an AD-assisted implicit derivative for regularised smooth contacts and apply it to Mujoco MJX, based on the Implicit Function Theorem (IFT). The method differentiates the stationarity residual at the tolerance-converged solution, avoiding both solver unrolling and hand-assembled KKT systems. IFT keeps compiled temporary memory nearly constant with solver effort, changing by less than 4$\%$ from one to ten iterations versus 10.6$\times$ growth for unrolled AD. IFT memory grows slower with active contacts and model dimension, using 20$\times$ less memory at 256 contacts and 6$\times$ less at 16 contacts and 96 DoF. We further introduce optimiser distillation for residual MPC, amortising batched full-horizon iLQR into a policy that guides short-horizon residual iLQR. Across Finger, Franka, and Unitree, this raises six-step success by 28-98 percentage points over standard iLQR.

</details>


### [121] [FIRMGrasp: A Friction-Informed Risk Margin for Robust Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25049)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FIRMGrasp，一种结合摩擦不确定性的抓取质量评估指标，能更准确地评估机械手抓取的稳定性风险。通过大规模实验验证，该方法优于传统评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的抓取质量指标通常假设摩擦系数是确定的，无法反映实际摩擦波动对抓取稳定性的影响。因此需要一种能反映摩擦不确定性的抓取质量度量方法，以更可靠地判断抓取是否真的稳固。

Method: 提出FIRMGrasp指标，引入Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) 风险度量，计算摩擦分布尾部的风险调整力闭包裕量 ε^{(β)}。证明该指标在信心水平β下的单调性、参数可微性，并给出概率闭包证书。与经典Ferrari-Canny指标和近年的可微分方法进行对比实验。

Result: 实验证明，在1599组不同的机械手抓取样本中，经典Ferrari-Canny方法认证的抓取有53%在恶劣摩擦条件下会失去力闭包，而FIRMGrasp指标能更好区分成功与失败的抓取，在实际摇晃和提取实验中的预测准确率高于传统方法。

Conclusion: FIRMGrasp能有效识别摩擦敏感抓取且风险容忍性更高，能为抓取任务提供更可靠的质量评价，是比经典方法更实用的抓取质量指标。

Abstract: Classical grasp quality metrics assume a single deterministic friction coefficient, so they cannot predict whether a grasp retains force closure across the range of friction values the contacting surfaces may exhibit. To predict these failures, we present FIRMGrasp, a family of friction-volatility-aware grasp quality metrics grounded in the Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) risk measure. Unlike standard grasp quality assessors that assume a single friction realization, our metric evaluates the force-closure margin at the CVaR-discounted mean of the adverse friction tail, yielding a risk-adjusted margin $\varepsilon^{(β)}$, the inscribed-ball radius of the risk-adjusted wrench space. We establish its monotonicity in the confidence level $β$, its differentiability in the grasp parameters, and a probabilistic closure certificate that guarantees force closure with probability at least $β$ whenever $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ is positive. Under a calibrated friction distribution, analytic evaluation shows our $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ metric identifies friction-sensitive grasps that the nominal Ferrari-Canny epsilon rates as high-quality, and we compare against the nominal epsilon and recent differentiable baselines. Across 1,599 LEAP Hand and Allegro Hand grasps, 53% of the grasps the nominal Ferrari-Canny margin certifies lose force closure in the adverse friction tail. On the same set, the nominal margin separates realized shake and pick success with probabilities of only 0.53 and 0.67, near chance on shake success, whereas $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ orders the pair correctly with probabilities of 0.63 and 0.78, respectively. In simulated lift trials with gravity enabled at an adverse friction coefficient of 0.2, grasps $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ certifies reach a 70% success rate under lateral pull, against 25% for grasps the nominal margin certifies but $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ rejects.

</details>


### [122] [Motion Generation With Environmental Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25053)
*Előd Páll,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过与环境有意识地接触（ECE方法），以简化机器人运动规划在高维空间和不确定环境中复杂性的新思路。实验结果显示，该方法提高了规划效率和机器人执行鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人运动规划在高维和不确定环境中需要避免碰撞，导致计算复杂度高，难以适应复杂情境。本文动机在于找到更有效的运动规划方法，让机器人更高效适应实际环境。

Method: 作者提出在运动规划中主动利用与环境的接触（ECE），通过在路径搜索时将接触视为减少不确定性和降低问题维度的手段，特别结合了RRT（快速随机树）等规划算法，并对比传统不接触策略。

Result: 应用ECE方法的RRT规划器能更聚焦于与任务相关的环境区域，通过实际案例展示了该方法在真实场景中的优越性，提升了运动规划的适应性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了合理利用环境接触能够有效简化机器人运动规划问题，提高其对复杂和不确定环境的适应能力，为今后的相关研究和实际应用提供了有益参考。

Abstract: Robot motion planning faces challenges in high-dimensional spaces and uncertain environments, often constrained by the need for collision-free motions. We advocate an alternative approach, Environmental Constraint Exploitation (ECE), where deliberate contact with the environment simplifies planning by reducing dimensionality and computational complexity. By integrating ECE into motion planning algorithms, we bias exploration to task-relevant regions and leverage contact for uncertainty reduction to improve robustness during execution. We evaluate ECE benefits with RRT-based planners and demonstrate their practical benefits in a real-world application. This work consolidates and extends prior research, showcasing how ECE simplifies motion planning while enhancing adaptability and performance in complex environments.

</details>


### [123] [Hybrid Artificial Potential Fields and Spatio-Temporal Transformers for Real-Time AUV Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25056)
*Khadija Rais,Abdelmadjid Benmachiche,Imene Soualmia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对13种主流水下自主航行器（AUV）路径规划算法进行了对比评估，提出并验证了一种将人工势场（APF）与时空Transformer相结合的混合方法，展现出在路径最优性、避障和计算效率上的最佳平衡优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在复杂环境中执行任务时，路径规划既关乎安全也影响能源消耗，现实环境对算法的实时性、完备性和能效有严格要求。目前尚缺少系统对比各种经典及新兴方法的性能；同时，需要创新方法提升路径最优性与动态反应能力。

Method: 对A*、Dijkstra、RRT*等图搜索/采样法，粒子群、遗传算法等元启发式方法，以及深度学习相关模型进行了定量对比。重点提出并实验了APF与ST-Transformer相结合的混合方法，在高分辨率实际地形下的五种导航测试场景中综合测试其性能。

Result: 所有算法均能完成任务，但具体表现存在明显权衡：经典方法无碰撞但路径长、慢，学习方法需辅助机制，元启发式轨迹复杂且不节能。混合APF+ST-Transformer方法表现最优：平均最短路径（943.15）、低碰撞率（0.031）、高效率（0.96s）。

Conclusion: 混合APF+ST-Transformer方案兼顾了智能避障和整体路径优化，尤其适合AUV现实应用，推荐作为实时导航的主流方案，能在资源受限水下系统中实现高效安全导航。

Abstract: Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) operate in complex, unstructured environments where efficient and safe path planning is critical for mission success and energy conservation. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative evaluation of thirteen path planning algorithms, ranging from classical graph-search methods (A*, Dijkstra) and sampling-based approaches (RRT*) to metaheuristics (PSO, GA, ACO, BCO) and learning-based architectures. Special emphasis is placed on a proposed hybrid approach combining Artificial Potential Fields (APF) with a Spatio-Temporal (ST) Transformer. Evaluated across five navigation scenarios on high-resolution underwater terrain maps, all algorithms achieved 100\% task completion; however, significant trade-offs emerged in path optimality, collision avoidance, and computational load. The Hybrid APF + ST-Transformer demonstrated superior balanced performance, achieving the shortest average path length (943.15 units), a low collision rate (0.031), and efficient computation time (0.96 s), outperforming standalone learning models, which required fallback mechanisms and classical methods that incurred higher latency. While classical algorithms guaranteed collision-free paths, their excessive path lengths and processing times render them less suitable for dynamic underwater operations. Conversely, metaheuristic approaches introduced trajectory complexity unsuitable for strict energy constraints. Based on these findings, the Hybrid APF + ST framework is recommended as a principal approach for real-time AUV navigation, offering a robust solution that harmonizes reactive obstacle avoidance with global path optimality in resource-constrained underwater systems.

</details>


### [124] [Input Shaping for Point-to-Point Motion with a Continuum Robot Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25071)
*Rodolfo Hdz. Ibarra,Karan Baker,Parsa Molaei,Adrian Stein,Hunter B. Gilbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了利用时延滤波器作为输入整形器，以抑制缆驱柔性臂末端操作时的残余振动，并通过实验验证了方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆驱柔性机械臂由于欠驱特性，在末端从静止到静止运动时常出现残余振动，这影响了末端精度和系统稳定性，因此亟需减少或抑制这些振动。

Method: 设计了基于线性系统模型的非鲁棒和鲁棒型时延滤波器，并作为输入整形器应用于机械臂控制，抑制系统的振动模式，并通过实验与传统速度脉冲输入方式进行对比。

Result: 实验结果显示，加入时延滤波器后，系统超调和收敛时间显著减少，振动被有效抑制；鲁棒型滤波器的效果优于非鲁棒型。

Conclusion: 所提出的输入整形方法能够更精确、鲁棒地减小柔性机械臂末端的振动，推进了柔性机器人在复杂轨迹任务中的应用前景。

Abstract: A cable-driven continuum robot arm is an underactuated mechanism and may suffer residual vibration at the end of a rest-to-rest maneuver. In this work, a time-delay filter is applied as an input shaper to the system to eliminate the excitation of vibratory modes. A non-robust and a robust time-delay filter are designed based on a linear system model and demonstrate improved response compared to a velocity-driven pulse input. Experimental results using the continuum robot validate the application of the input shaper, with reduced overshoot and settling time exemplifying the reduction in oscillation at the end of the maneuver. It is also shown that utilizing the robust shaper further improves the response of the arm in comparison to applying the non-robust shaper. These results are significant towards the precise and robust implementation of continuum robots in applications involving arbitrary end-effector trajectories.

</details>


### [125] [Reactive 3D Motion Planning for a Franka Arm via Star-World Workspace Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25138)
*Gia Dcosta,Saayuj Deshpande,Samhitha Vedire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过Star-World空间重塑方法，将原本因安全膨胀导致相交的障碍物分组并用星形代理表示，从而提升机械臂在三维空间避障时与环境的适应性和安全性。对比试验显示该方法提升了到达目标的成功率，但路径长度增加、部分场景下出现近平衡点问题，且部分通道被过度合并堵塞，显示出方法的优缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多基于调制的反应式路径规划方法假设障碍物互不相交，但实际中障碍物因安全膨胀后往往重叠，这会破坏算法预设的假设，影响避障性能。因此，研究如何在现实环境中优化膨胀后的障碍物表达，保证避障算法的有效性和鲁棒性成为关键问题。

Method: 采用对每次更新后的空间中，所有重叠并膨胀的障碍物进行聚类，用星形代理（star-shaped proxies）取代后，利用动态系统方法控制末端执行器。同时引入关节空间的虚拟力场辅助躯干避障。通过在PyBullet中的六个不同场景下，分别对重塑与未重塑障碍表达进行目标达成率、路径长度比和计算耗时等指标的评价。

Result: 实验显示，障碍物重塑方法在六个场景下有五个成功到达目标，而未重塑基线方法只有四个。同时解决了典型的重叠墙障碍物问题，并且每次工作空间更新的计算开销较小。但重塑方法会导致路径变长、部分场景出现近平衡点，且有时因过度合并而堵死可通行通道。

Conclusion: Star-World空间重塑方法可以帮助冗余机械臂在复杂三维环境中提升避障成功率，但同时带来路径效率下降以及可导航区域被误合并的局限，提示该方法在理论到实际应用转化时需进一步权衡和优化。

Abstract: Safety inflation can cause nearby obstacles to overlap, violating the disjoint-obstacle assumptions used by many modulation-based reactive planners. We investigate Star-World workspace reshaping for three-dimensional reactive control of a Franka Emika Panda manipulator. At each update, intersecting inflated obstacles are clustered and replaced by star-shaped proxies before a dynamical-system-based end-effector controller is evaluated. A null-space artificial-potential-field term provides complementary arm-body avoidance. We compare reshaped and unreshaped obstacle representations in six PyBullet scenarios using goal attainment, path-length ratio, and computation time. In this preliminary 12-trial evaluation, reshaping reaches the goal in five of six scenarios, compared with four of six for the unreshaped baseline. It resolves the canonical overlapping-wall case and requires 0.68--8.70\,ms per workspace update for scenes containing one to seven obstacles. However, it also increases path length, produces near-equilibria in two cases, and closes a navigable corridor through over-aggressive merging. These results show both the promise and the practical limitations of transferring Star-World guarantees from workspace geometry to a redundant manipulator controlled through inverse kinematics.

</details>


### [126] [Leveraging Semantic Maps for City-Scale Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25215)
*Ethan Fahnestock,Erick Fuentes,Philip R Osteen,Nicholas Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用OpenStreetMap语义信息提升机器人在未知环境中定位的算法，通过蒸馏轻量级匹配器，实现了在大规模地图条件下的高效语义关联，并验证了方法对不同环境、天气和光照的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于公开地图数据的机器人定位方法对丰富的语义信息利用不足，限制了在复杂或未知环境中的应用。作者希望提高机器人利用开放数据在未遍历区域自主定位的能力。

Method: 方法一方面利用视觉语言模型(VLM)从机器人全景观测中提取地标语义，另一方面提出将VLM蒸馏为轻量级的实体匹配器，解决大规模地图下语义关联的效率瓶颈。最终将匹配结果与贝叶斯滤波结合，实现机器人位姿随时间的概率估计。

Result: 算法在包含11种环境（如波士顿雪天和夜间采集）的大型测试集上，展现了仅用单城市晴天数据训练下的强泛化能力，在不同地区、天气和照明条件下均能准确实现自主定位。

Conclusion: 论文证明了语义信息和轻量匹配器的结合极大提升了机器人跨视角与跨条件定位的能力，并为后续相关研究开放了数据集和代码资源。

Abstract: We want robots to localize in previously untraversed environments against commonly available prior data. Rich semantic data available from OpenStreetMap can be useful in this task. However, existing methods either ignore this semantic information, directly matching panoramas and overhead imagery, or dramatically compress the semantic information, working with a small set of fixed classes. To leverage this rich semantic information, two challenges need to be overcome. First, useful semantic information needs to be extracted from the robot's egocentric observations. Second, the observed information must be quickly associated with the large prior semantic map (e.g., up to 628 km^2). We show that VLMs are effective at both extracting relevant landmarks from panoramas, and identifying feasible correspondences between these landmarks and prior overhead landmarks. However, using VLMs to propose all correspondences scales poorly as the number of mapped landmarks increases. Instead, we propose distilling a lightweight matcher from a VLM which computes correspondences for all entities in a map. We use this output to form an observation likelihood which is fused over time with a Bayes filter to create a time series of pose estimates. To support further investigation into generalizable cross-view methods that leverage semantic information, we release a dataset of extracted semantics and evaluation trajectories spanning eleven environments, including panoramas we collected in a snowstorm and at night in Boston. We demonstrate our method, trained on a single city's fair-weather data, generalizes across location, lighting, weather, and other challenges. Code and datasets are available at https://efahnestock.github.io/loci/.

</details>


### [127] [Belief-Aware Influence and Trust (BAIT): Shaping Human Belief During Repeated Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25327)
*Ye-Ji Mun,Mahsa Golchoubian,Shahabedin Sagheb,Yan Bai,Tianhao Ji,Dylan P. Losey,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了BAIT控制器，以提升长时间人机交互中的任务表现与人类信任，优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互方法要么把每次互动视为孤立事件，导致人类认知偏移和任务表现下降，要么通过不可预测行为维持长期影响，但这样会损害用户信任并面临计算不可扩展问题。因此有必要提出新方法，兼顾影响力、信任与任务表现。

Method: 作者提出了BAIT（Belief-Aware Influence and Trust）控制器，将层次粒子滤波器用于推断人类策略变化和感知信念更新，并与基于信念的模型预测路径积分规划器结合，明确地优化长远影响与信任间的权衡，同时严格约束即时任务达成。

Result: 通过仿真、人类实验与真实车辆场景实验，BAIT在反复并线任务中与当前优化长期影响力的基线方法相比，任务表现相当，但用户信任度显著提升。

Conclusion: BAIT控制器有效提升了长期人机交互中的信任和任务表现，为信念感知型多轮交互提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Repeated human-robot interaction (HRI) requires proactively accounting for humans who continually adapt to evolving beliefs about the robot. Prior frameworks often treat encounters as isolated events, suffering cumulative task performance decay as human perception drifts, or maintain long-term influence through erratic, unpredictable behavior that erodes perceived human trust and relies on computationally unscalable formulations. To address these gaps, we introduce the Belief- Aware Influence and Trust (BAIT) controller. BAIT integrates a hierarchical particle filter, which infers both fast human strategic shifts and slow perceptual belief updates, with a belief-aware Model Predictive Path Integral planner. BAIT explicitly optimizes the trade-off between long-horizon influence and human trust, while enforcing immediate task performance as a strict constraint. Across simulations, a human-subject study, and a real-world GEM vehicle deployments in repeated lane-merging scenarios, BAIT achieves task performance comparable to baselines that optimize long-term influence through unpredictability while yielding significantly higher user trust. The video demonstrating our experiments is available at https://youtu.be/GsPfHRujzVs.

</details>


### [128] [SGTP: Sampling-based Game-Theoretic Planning for Real-Time Multi-Vehicle Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25388)
*Zhouheng Li,Fangguo Zhao,Mattia Piccinini,Baha Zarrouki,Yuan Gao,Zitong Shan,Johannes Betz,Chen Lv,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新颖的采样式博弈规划（SGTP）方法，实现多车自主竞速中的实时多样化竞技策略规划，并在高互动环境下取得了优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多车竞速规划器在策略多样性与计算效率间难以兼顾，限制了真实竞速环境下的表现。为提升多车互动中的自主决策能力，亟需既能高效计算、又能丰富策略输出的新方法。

Method: 提出SGTP框架，将博弈理论推理与GPU加速采样控制序列及动力学预测相结合，通过博弈感知代价对采样轨迹排序，捕捉复杂竞争互动，并依次筛选可行解，严格遵守赛道和避障约束，确保安全和策略切换。

Result: SGTP在高难度赛道仿真中取得95.24%胜率和99.35%的任务完成率，平均每步运算时间仅0.095秒，并能支持多至10辆车的大规模竞速环境。

Conclusion: SGTP展示了在多车自主竞速中高效、稳定且策略多样的实时规划能力，为未来多智能体竞速研究提供了公开代码与基准，有力推动了领域发展。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-vehicle racing requires real-time planning of diverse competitive behaviors in intense interactions. Existing planners often struggle to balance strategic diversity and computational efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose Sampling-based Game-Theoretic Planning (SGTP), a real-time framework that combines game-theoretic reasoning with GPU-accelerated sampling of control sequences and dynamics rollouts. Sampled trajectories are ranked using a game-aware cost to capture competitive interactions and generate diverse racing behaviors. Our planner then performs feasibility selection by explicitly enforcing track-boundary and dynamic collision-avoidance constraints, ensuring safe and reliable transitions between racing strategies. Extensive simulations on challenging tracks show that SGTP achieves a 95.24% win rate and a 99.35% task-completion ratio in highly interactive races, with a mean computational time of 0.095 s over multiple iterative solving steps. We also demonstrate the successful application of SGTP in large-scale scenarios with up to 10 agents. We release our code and provide an open-source benchmark of multi-agent autonomous racing algorithms to facilitate future research. Project page: https://sgtp-racing.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [Decompose and Reorganize: Planning with Primitives and Visuomotor Policies Learned from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25397)
*Yizhou Chen,Hang Xu,Dongjie Yu,Yupu Lu,Tengye Xu,Zeqing Zhang,Wei Zhang,Yi Ren,Ben M. Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DR-LfD的新框架，将类人操作的演示分解为细粒度的原子技能，并通过与任务与运动规划（TAMP）系统的集成，实现多步骤和复杂约束下的灵巧机器人操作自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的任务与运动规划（TAMP）在高层计划上表现优秀，但在涉及复杂接触的操作时容易失效，而模仿学习（IL）虽然在具体操作上适应性好，但无法很好地泛化到不同空间布局和多步骤任务。两者各有优缺，亟需一种框架能兼顾高层推理与低层操作。

Method: DR-LfD框架首先基于接触关系将人类演示分解为原子技能，然后将这些技能以视觉-运动策略或以对象为中心的方式实现。通过对这些策略的启动、终止和约束进行建模，将其转为可与TAMP兼容的模块，从而实现技能的重组和复用。该方法极大降低了演示数据的需求，将学习难度从涉及所有技能组合的指数级减少到只与独特技能类型数量成比例。

Result: 通过多样化的真实场景与仿真测试验证，DR-LfD在多步骤任务、全新环境和具有物理约束的场景中均展现出优异表现，远超传统TAMP和单一模仿学习方法。

Conclusion: DR-LfD有效结合了TAMP的高层决策能力和模仿学习的灵巧操作优势，实现了复杂任务下的技能自动化迁移和复用，为未来灵巧机器人操作提供了通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Successfully automating dexterous, long-horizon robotic manipulation requires frameworks capable of both high-level reasoning and fine-grained execution. Traditional task and motion planning (TAMP), while excellent at symbolic planning, is often brittle in contact-rich operations. Simultaneously, imitation learning (IL), while effective in manipulation tasks with visual feedback, is limited by its low capability in spatial generalization and multi-stage operation. To reconcile their complementary strengths and limitations, we propose DR-LfD (Decomposed and Reorganized Skills Learned from Demonstrations), a framework that seamlessly integrates visuomotor policies into a TAMP-gated decision-making system. Based on contact relationships, DR-LfD decomposes human demonstrations into atomic skills, which are reproduced as visuomotor policies or object-centric primitives. The initiation, termination, and constraints of the visuomotor policies are carefully modeled and implemented in a TAMP-compatible form, enabling reorganization of skills learned from different sources. DR-LfD transforms the learning problem from one requiring exponential demonstration data over possible skill sequences to one whose demonstration burden scales with the number of distinct skill types, with limited data for each skill. Through comprehensive real-world and simulation benchmarking across diverse scenarios, we demonstrate the strong performance of DR-LfD on tasks involving multiple steps, unseen setups, and physical constraints. Project website: https://dr-lfd.github.io/DR-LfD-website.

</details>


### [130] [Room-Mediated Co-occurrence for Zero-Shot Object-Centric Semantic Navigation via Frontier Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25448)
*Adam Scicluna,Gavin Paul,Alen Alempijevic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于房间词汇表的零样本目标导航（Zero-shot ObjectNav）管道，通过分析和融合对象之间的语义关系，无需训练即可实现高效、可解释的物体导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉-语言模型的Zero-shot ObjectNav方法往往仅利用对象在模型隐空间的相似度，而忽略了空间共现关系，这种方法难以有效捕捉对象在真实场景中的空间分布规律，因此需要设计更合理的对象语义关联方式。

Method: 首先为每个对象标签生成一个基于CLIP的房间概率向量（RPV），通过RPV分布重叠度计算对象与目标的共现概率。再结合地质泛洪（Fast Marching Method）传播和自适应信号衰减，将语义分数映射到全局地图上，用于引导前沿选择和导航路径规划。整体方案不需要训练，且以对象为中心。

Result: 在HM3D数据集验证集上，相较于基于图像全局表征的baseline，本文方法在Success Rate（SR）和SPL（Success weighted by Path Length）上分别提升了3%和1.3%，同时保持了解释性和开集词汇泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入训练无关、对象中心的房间概率语义表达，可以显著提升zero-shot语义导航的效果和解释性，为后续相关研究奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: Zero-shot ObjectNav methods increasingly use vision-language priors, but direct object-object similarity in the latent space is often a weak proxy for spatial co-occurrence. We present an analytical, training-free semantic navigation pipeline that mediates object relationships through a compact room lexicon. Each object label is mapped to a CLIP-derived Room Probability Vector (RPV), and object-target co-occurrence is computed from RPV distribution overlap. These scores are projected onto a value map using geodesic flood-fill propagation (Fast Marching Method), with adaptive signal decay, and are used to rank frontiers by semantic score for navigation. Together, these components form an integrated, training-free, object-centric pipeline for open-vocabulary zero-shot navigation. Results show that our object-centric approach improves Success Rate (SR) and Success by weighted inverse Path Length (SPL) by a relative 3% and 1.3%, respectively, compared to image-holistic baselines on the HM3D dataset validation split, while preserving interpretability and open-vocabulary flexibility. Code is available at: uts-ri.github.io/RPV-SemNav.

</details>


### [131] [A Causality-aware Infer-diagnose-refine Framework for Test-time Modality Adaptation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25516)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Jin Chen,Gao Zhi,Zhenxin Diao,Mingyang Gao,Kun Wu,Yongchun Liu,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一个新的模型不可知的推理-诊断-优化（IDR）框架，用于改进视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作时对动作的预测精度，尤其是针对视觉信息动态重要性的调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操作任务中，视觉、语言和动作的多模态融合非常关键，但由于操作过程包括远距离移动和近距离交互等动态阶段，因此视觉信息的重要性在时序上不断变化。当前VLA模型尚未有效地调优不同阶段视觉信息的重要性。

Method: 作者提出IDR框架，可集成到任何VLA模型中，无需额外训练，仅在测试阶段调整。具体方法为：1）在实际和反事实的视觉观测下分别推理动作；2）诊断视觉观测对动作的因果影响，量化其动态重要性；3）基于诊断结果优化最终动作。实现方面包括零填充干预、基于范数的因果效应量化及门控残差融合模块。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实任务中，跨多种主流VLA架构嵌入IDR后性能均有提升，证明了测试时动态调整视觉信息重要性的效果。

Conclusion: IDR框架能有效根据任务阶段动态调整视觉观测的权重，从而提升VLA模型对复杂机器人任务的动作预测能力，并且方法通用、无须额外训练，具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict sequential actions to execute tasks specified by language instructions, conditioned on visual observations and proprioceptive states. However, how to fuse modalities in VLA models remains an open problem, since robot manipulation involves dynamic phases, such as long-distance movements and close-range interactions, in which the importance of visual observations may vary over time. In this paper, we propose an infer-diagnose-refine (IDR) framework, a model-agnostic framework that can be integrated with diverse VLA architectures for refining action predictions at test time. IDR first infers actions under factual and counterfactual scenarios of visual observations, and then diagnoses the causal effects of visual observations as the estimated dynamic importance, which is finally used to refine the action predictions in a training-free manner. We further design a causality-aware action refiner to realize the IDR framework, including zero-padding interventions for inferring counterfactual actions, norm-based quantification for diagnosing causal effects, and gated residual fusion for refining actions. Extensive experiments on both simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show improvements in overall performance across multiple VLA backbones, demonstrating the efficacy of dynamically adjusting visual importance at test time.

</details>


### [132] [P3: Probabilistic Policy Propagation for Stable VAE-Based Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25541)
*Liyun Yan,Jianming Ma,Yang Zhang,Shengcheng Fu,Zhanxiang Cao,Keqi Zhu,Yizhi Chen,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了变分自编码器（VAE）在机器人控制中与PPO算法结合时的问题，并提出了新的优化框架P^3，显著提升了数据效率和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE在处理高维噪声观测方面表现优异，但其与PPO结合时，因只用单一潜变量样本估算概率比与KL散度，导致近似偏差和高方差，影响策略优化。

Method: 提出P^3框架，结合基于矩的概率方法实现高效学习，并用采样校准提升潜在不确定性下的策略鲁棒性。该框架针对VAE结合PPO的代理损失中的方差和偏差问题进行理论与算法改进。

Result: P^3大幅提升数据效率（64.6%提升至96%以上），减少收敛步数超过20%；在困难的人形障碍任务中展现出色表现。

Conclusion: P^3为VAE与PPO的结合提供了有效的理论基础和算法提升，有效解决了原生方法的方差与偏差问题，在机器人高级控制任务中具有较大应用潜力。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders are widely used to encode high-dimensional and noisy observations in robotics. However, their stochastic latent creates a mismatch with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO): an effective policy marginalizes over the latent distribution, whereas former implementations estimate its probability ratio and KL divergence using only one latent sample. We identify a fundamental but overlooked theoretical cause: naive single-sample approximations in stochastic latent space induce significant variance and bias in the surrogate loss. To address this, we introduce P^3 (Probabilistic Policy Propagation), a distribution-aware optimization framework for VAE-based policies. $P^3$ couples moment-based probabilistic method for stable and efficient learning with sampling-based calibration for robust policy behavior under latent uncertainty. In our experiments, P^3 boosts data efficiency from 64.6% to >96%, reduces convergence steps by >20%. Furthermore, P^3 is evaluated on challenging humanoid parkour tasks and shows an effective foundation for VAE-based PPO. Code is available at https://github.com/ylyem9x/P3_Open.

</details>


### [133] [When Does Legacy Data Start to Help? Emergent Transfer in Cross-Configuration Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25593)
*Tao Wang,Hudson Hou,Yingdong Hu,Yufeng Liu,Qinghai Li,Yingjie Jiang,Yingzhi Wang,Cheng Ma,Richard Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了旧硬件演示数据何时对升级后的机器人有益，并提出数据迁移存在“阈值”现象：机器人需具备一定任务能力后，才能有效利用历史数据，从而带来快速性能提升，但进步在更高水平后趋于饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人硬件不断升级，现有演示数据往往与特定传感器和执行器绑定。这导致难以直接利用历史数据提升新硬件表现。作者关注的是实际问题：在硬件升级后，历史数据何时真正对新系统有帮助？

Method: 作者在一个轮式人形机器人平台上（跨越两代硬件，摄像头和夹持器发生变化），通过实际操作任务，系统研究了“跨硬件配置数据迁移”的效果，并将实验结果与理论分析结合。

Result: 作者发现并验证了“迁移三阶段”：1）任务能力低时，旧数据几乎无帮助；2）一旦新硬件达到“迁移阈值”，旧数据能迅速提升表现（如花插入任务从23.3%跃升到86.7%）；3）能力高时，旧数据收益逐渐降低（如笔插入任务从85.0%提升到93.3%）。理论上以梯度对齐及策略不确定性解释这一现象，并给出何时采集新数据，何时用旧数据的公式。

Conclusion: 硬件迁移数据的有效性高度依赖于新硬件达到一定的任务能力。仅在跨越能力阈值后，历史数据才能带来显著增益，初期和高能力阶段收益有限。三阶段模式可指导实际机器人数据采集与迁移策略。

Abstract: Robotic hardware evolves over time, but demonstration data is often tied to a specific sensor and actuator configuration. This raises a practical and underexplored question: when does legacy data begin to benefit an upgraded robot? We study this question on a wheeled humanoid platform across two hardware generations, where both the camera and gripper are changed while the overall morphology remains fixed. Contrary to the common assumption that more cross-configuration data is always helpful, we observe a grokking-like transition: legacy data remains ineffective until the upgraded configuration acquires a minimum level of task competence, after which co-training gains rise sharply before diminishing near saturation. We hypothesize that this task-dependent transition is governed by a transfer threshold and characterize the resulting three-phase pattern. Across real-robot manipulation tasks, we observe all three phases: no measurable benefit at low competence ($10.0\% \rightarrow 10.0\%$), a sharp gain after crossing the threshold ($23.3\% \rightarrow 86.7\%$ on flower insertion), and diminishing returns at high competence ($85.0\% \rightarrow 93.3\%$ on pen insertion). We provide a theoretical account based on gradient alignment and residual policy uncertainty, and derive a phase-aware rule for deciding when to collect more new-hardware data and when to reuse legacy demonstrations. We further validate this three-phase pattern on a mobile dual-arm watering task, with results consistent with our predictions.

</details>


### [134] [Shared Voxel-Map-Based Cooperative Indoor UAV Guidance with a Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25728)
*Thomas Hickling,Dylan Wynne,Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将共享体素地图与多智能体软演员-评论家（MASAC）控制器结合的室内无人机协作引导框架，实现了高效和可扩展的多无人机室内导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的室内多无人机协作导航存在空间信息融合难、控制分散难以协调等问题，且在GNSS-denied环境下尤为突出。需要一种高效融合空间认知与去中心化控制的解决方案。

Method: 多架无人机通过360度激光雷达观测，共同构建体素占据图并生成鸟瞰视角（BEV）局部视野，作为每架无人机的输入。采用MASAC实现中央训练、分布式执行，输入组合包括BEV局部地图、近场障碍、目标和队友状态信息。为缩小仿真与实际差距，进一步用实地数据进行离线模仿微调。

Result: 在仿真中，该学习控制器模块走廊导航成功率达90.3%，超越了Astar规划、势场法和既有方法。实地测试表明，在无GNSS环境下，两架无人机在复杂障碍布局下协作稳定运行。

Conclusion: 共享体素地图为学习型多无人机协作提供了有效、可扩展的空间基础。该方法在实际室内复杂环境下表现出良好的协作与适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a cooperative indoor UAV guidance framework that combines a shared voxel-map world model with a multi-agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC) controller. Multiple drones fuse 360 LiDAR observations into a common world-frame occupancy map, which is converted into a compact bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation and provided to each agent as an ego-aligned local crop. This integrate-in-world, act-in- ego design enables consistent multi-UAV spatial fusion whilst retaining decentralised continuous control. The policy combines BEV map features, near-field obstacle observations, and compact goal and peer-state information within a centralised-training, decentralised-execution framework. In simulation, the learned controller achieves a 90.3% success rate in corridor navigation, outperforming Astar planning, an artificial potential field controller, and a prior guidance method. To address residual sim-to-real mismatch, the simulation-trained policy is further adapted using offline imitation fine-tuning from real-world data. Real-world experiments in GNSS-denied indoor environments demonstrate stable two-UAV cooperative operation across increasingly chal- lenging obstacle layouts. The results show that shared voxel-map representations provide an effective and scalable spatial substrate for learned cooperative indoor UAV guidance.

</details>


### [135] [Tri-Manual Visuomotor Imitation Learning of Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25731)
*James Zhao,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三机械臂模仿学习系统TriManPolicy，解决了操作员只能同时操控两臂、三臂需切换模式而引入人为延迟的问题，通过依赖感知的时间重排方法提升三臂协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双臂遥操作假设人机控制通道数量一致，但在三臂系统中，单人无法连续同时操控三臂，常采用切换模式，导致动作顺序与任务需求不符，引入非必要延迟，这影响模仿学习的效果。需要新方法让单人演示三臂高效协同动作。

Method: 提出TriManPolicy模仿学习系统，核心为DATS（依赖感知三臂调度）算法。该方法离线地对演示数据按传感/运动段切分，结合任务顺序和手臂使用的约束，在人工确认下重排演示中各动作的发生时机，保留每只手臂的独立运动序列，生成同步三臂训练数据，用于训练单一并行策略。

Result: 在六项真实世界三臂任务中，用DATS重排后的演示训练出的策略比原始方法有更高的协同效率，且任务完成率保持可比水平。离线分析进一步证实DATS改变了各臂的监督方式，而不仅是简单去除等待时间。

Conclusion: TriManPolicy通过DATS重排演示，有效缓解了三臂控制的接口性延迟，使单操作者能高效示教三臂机器人，提升整个模仿学习系统的效率，且实际表现优良。

Abstract: Bimanual teleoperation provides an effective way to collect robot demonstrations, but it assumes that the operator and robot have matching numbers of simultaneous control channels. This assumption breaks for tri-manual systems: the robot can coordinate three arms concurrently, whereas a single operator can continuously control only two. Pairwise mode switching may therefore record otherwise independent motions sequentially, causing behaviour cloning to reproduce delays imposed by the interface rather than required by the task. We present TriManPolicy, a tri-manual imitation learning system that allows one operator to demonstrate behaviours for three arms. Its central component is Dependency-Aware Tri-Arm Scheduling (DATS). The key idea is to preserve the demonstrated arm motions while reconsidering when they occur. DATS retimes demonstrations offline by preserving local sensorimotor segments of fixed duration and repositioning them according to constraints on task order and arm usage that are reviewed by a human. The resulting data train a single synchronous policy for all three arms, while deployment requires neither the dependency graph nor the scheduler. Across six challenging tasks performed in the real world, policies trained on demonstrations retimed by DATS exhibit more efficient coordination while maintaining comparable observed task success. Offline analysis further shows that DATS changes the supervision across arms rather than merely removing idle periods.

</details>


### [136] [Cooperative Multi-UAV Navigation in Complex Environments via Systematic Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25754)
*Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体深度强化学习框架，通过创新机制提升无人机协同导航在复杂环境中的性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多无人机协同导航面临：陷入局部最优、奖励稀疏、学习不均衡及跨场景泛化能力弱等问题，急需有效解决方案提升协作效率与环境适应性。

Method: 1）结合记忆、方向新颖性和惩罚反向传播的感知机制帮助规避局部最优；2）分级协作演示缓存与差异化行为克隆强化示例利用；3）基于安全的课程调度减少灾难性遗忘，同时保障训练中飞行安全与任务表现；4）通过本地结构特征驱动的专家混合机制，实现基于结构的泛化策略，从而无需目标场景数据即可迁移。

Result: 在包含静态与动态障碍的混合环境中仿真验证，所提方法在协作成功率、导航鲁棒性、零样本跨场景泛化及动态环境适应性等方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提多智能体强化学习框架显著提升了无人机协同导航任务的通用性和可靠性，为复杂环境下的实际部署提供了理论与方法支持。

Abstract: Cooperative navigation of multi-agent UAVs in complex environments faces key challenges including local optima traps, sparse rewards, learning imbalance among agents, and insufficient cross-scenario generalisation. This paper proposes a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework that addresses these issues through coordinated exploration, demonstration exploitation, safe curriculum scheduling, and structure-aware generalisation. First, a perception mechanism combining memory of visited states, directional novelty estimates, and penalty backpropagation enables agents to proactively detect and escape local optima. Second, a hierarchical collaborative demonstration buffer with tiered behaviour cloning manages trajectories by degree of team collaboration and applies differential supervision to the actor network, improving demonstration utilisation under sparse collaborative signals. Third, a safety-aware dual-condition curriculum scheduling mechanism reviews mastered scenarios through back-testing and experience pre-filling during training, suppressing catastrophic forgetting while ensuring both task performance and flight safety. For generalisation, local geometric features computed from sensor readings are abstracted into a domain parameter, through which a structure-aware gating network and mixture-of-experts mechanism condition the policy on local structural patterns rather than scenario-specific coordinates, enabling cross-scenario transfer without exposure to the target environment. The framework is further validated under mixed static-dynamic obstacle settings, showing robust adaptability to dynamic disturbances. Simulation results confirm strong performance in collaboration success rate, navigation robustness, zero-shot cross-scenario generalisation, and dynamic environment adaptability.

</details>


### [137] [Tripody: An Overconstrained 3-SPR-like Parallel Robot for High-Reach Construction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25781)
*Julien Kindle,Jakub Raczy,Riccardo Balbi,Andrea Alessandretti,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量化的三自由度并联机器人Tripody，适用于天花板等高处作业，具备高刚性和毫米级定位精度。该机器人结构创新，采用过约束机制来提升性能，实验表明其具有出色的负载能力和定位精度，能够胜任实际施工任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的天花板施工多依赖于笨重的串联机械臂，在狭小空间内部署困难，因此亟需开发能够达到天花板高度、具备高精度与高刚性的轻量化现场机器人。

Method: 设计了一种基于3-SPR架构改进的并联机器人Tripody，用万向节代替球形关节形成过约束，配合弹性小变形吸收不兼容性。系统采用定制线性执行器、模块化末端工具，并开发了结合SE(3)状态估计、前向运动学和任务空间控制的控制系统。通过力学分析和多种实验评估其性能。

Result: Tripody可伸缩高度范围为1.7m至3.4m，重量33kg，支持32kg连续载荷，末端工具模块化。实验显示其平面刚度与球基型结构相当，但扭转刚度提升大（1.7m处提升67%，3.4m提升454%），定位误差闭环低于0.6mm，模型推算误差95%在2.7mm以内。开放环钻孔实验的最大相对误差为4.5mm，验证了高精度施工能力。

Conclusion: 过约束、具备弹性吸收能力的3-SPR类并联机器人架构在实际工程中可作为轻量化、高伸展、毫米级精度建造机器人的一条可行路径。

Abstract: Many ceiling construction tasks still rely on heavy serial manipulators that are difficult to deploy in cluttered interiors, motivating lightweight, field-ready alternatives that reach ceiling height while maintaining millimeter-level accuracy and the stiffness demanded by overhead tool loads. We introduce Tripody, a wheeled 3-DoF parallel robot for high-reach tasks that replaces the base spherical joints of a classical 3-SPR (3 legs; S: base spherical joint; P: actuated prismatic joint; R: end-effector revolute joint) morphology with universal joints, intentionally overconstraining the mechanism; small, distributed elastic deflections absorb the resulting incompatibilities, preserving predominantly translational motion. The 33kg system extends from 1.7m to 3.4m in height, supports a continuous 32kg payload, and offers a modular end-effector interface for ceiling operations. We detail the mechanical design - including custom linear actuators and a kinematic-compatibility analysis - and a control stack for accurate positioning that combines SE(3) state estimation, forward kinematics, and task-space control. In experiments, Tripody exhibits similar in-plane stiffness to a spherical-base variant but substantially higher torsional stiffness - an increase of 67% at 1.7m, 196% at 2.6m, and 454% at 3.4m - while maintaining negligible cross-axis coupling. Closed-loop positioning with a total station converges below 0.6mm across the entire workspace; pure model extrapolation achieves a 95th-percentile error of 2.7mm (max 3.6mm). Finally, we demonstrate task-level ceiling-drilling feasibility in an open-loop study by drilling a 15-hole pattern with 4.5mm maximum relative hole-position error after rigid alignment. These results support overconstrained, compliance-absorbing 3-SPR-like architectures as a practical path to lightweight, high- reach, millimeter-accurate construction robots.

</details>


### [138] [Motion-Acceleration Calibration and Compensation in IMUs without External Equipment for Attitude Estimation Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25784)
*Fabian Arzberger,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过陀螺仪测量补偿由系统运动引起的离心和切向加速度，并改进IMU姿态估计算法的方法，同时引入了一种无需额外外部设备即可自校准IMU内外参数的新方法。该方法提升了基于重力方向姿态滤波器在高动态情况下的精确性，尤其适用于IMU无法安装于旋转中心的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动系统中，IMU受到运动引起的加速度影响，导致加速度计测得的重力方向偏离真实值。现有姿态估计算法在高速运动时主要依赖陀螺仪积分，易于积累误差，尤其是重力方向。本文希望解决高动态下姿态漂移和IMU安装位置带来的影响，提高姿态估计的精度和适用性。

Method: 1. 利用陀螺仪数据补偿运动引起的额外加速度（离心和切向加速度），提升IMU对重力方向的感知。
2. 提出一种自校准方法，可以估算IMU的内在参数（包括轴失准、偏置、比例因子）和外在参数（IMU到基座的安装矢量），无需外部设备辅助。
3. 通过仿真和真实环境实验对提出的方法进行评估。

Result: 实验证明所提出方法能提升任何依赖重力方向的姿态滤波器的精度，特别是在高动态系统和IMU难以安装于旋转中心的场合。论文在真实的球形移动测绘系统中展示了其有效性。

Conclusion: 本文方法能够有效抑制高动态运动和IMU偏离旋转中心时的姿态估计误差，为移动系统提供更精准和更易部署的姿态感知方案，提升了现有滤波器算法的可靠性和适应性。

Abstract: Attitude estimation based on inertial sensing requires measurements of local angular velocities and local gravity via gyroscopes and accelerometers.
  However, during the motion of a mobile system the inertial measurement unit (IMU) will be subject to additional accelerations which skews the measurement of local gravity.
  This effect gets amplified the further away the IMU is from the base of the system.
  Many attitude estimation filters, such as "Madgwick" or "Mahony", account for this by relying more on gyroscope integration for periods of high angular velocity.
  However, this approach is prone to accumulate long term error especially around the gravity vector.
  In this work we utilize the gyroscope measurements to compensate the additional accelerations induced by the motion of the system, i.e., centripetal- and tangential-accelerations.
  Additionally, we introduce a calibration method that estimates intrinsic IMU parameters such as axes misalignment, bias, scale, as well as the extrinsic base-to-IMU vector without the necessity for additional external equipment.
  Our evaluation in simulation as well as in the real-world shows that this method improves any attitude filter that relies on the direction of gravity.
  Furthermore we demonstrate the effectivenes on highly dynamic systems, and systems that are unable to put the IMU at the center of rotation, using our real-world spherical mobile mapping system.

</details>


### [139] [Transformer Transformer: A Unified Model for Motion-Conditioned Robot Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25798)
*Huy Ha,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散变换器的新型机器人共设计方法，可在不同机器人结构和应用任务间实现泛化。方法利用统一的RoboTokens表示机器人本体、状态与动作，支持在多种奖励和目标下生成高性能机器人设计，并通过Dynamics Self-Guidance机制实现高效优化。实验证明方法在多个设计空间和目标 reward 上实现零样本优化，比进化基线有显著提升，实物测试展现出70%以上误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人设计往往忽视机器人自身的结构对运动控制和任务完成度的影响，当前方法难以兼容多种机器人形态且易受特定奖励函数过拟合，缺乏高效泛化设计工具。本文旨在解决该痛点，实现运动与结构协同优化的机器人自动生成。

Method: 作者提出Transformer Transformer模型，将机器人结构、状态与动作统一编码为RoboTokens，并采用扩散机制训练。模型不直接针对具体奖励拟合，而是作为动力学模型输出奖励无关的状态与动作预测，再通过转换得到任何指定 reward 的价值预测。进而提出Dynamics Self-Guidance方法，用该价值预测引导扩散过程，优化出高性能的机器人设计。

Result: 实验涵盖多种机器人结构（如轮式、四足、人形）和多种任务，表明方法可在未见过的 reward 和目标轨迹下零样本优化，性能与运行时间均优于进化算法基线。实际对ALOHA机器人进行共设计后，轨迹跟踪误差降低超过70%。

Conclusion: 提出的方法不仅能针对不同形态和任务高效生成机器人设计，还能显著提升运动表现和泛化能力，相较传统进化共设计方法有明显优势，为机器人设计与控制协同优化提供新思路。

Abstract: An often overlooked factor of robot manipulation performance is the embodiment of the robot itself. Motivated by this problem, we study motion-conditioned robot co-design, where the goal is to generate complete robot designs that track target end-effector trajectories (from human demonstrations) while optimizing user-defined rewards. We introduce Transformer Transformer, a diffusion transformer trained on RoboTokens, a unified tokenization of robot embodiments, states, and actions. The same architecture can be used across embodiment spaces (e.g., wheeled bimanual, quadrupeds, humanoids) and use cases (embodiment generation, cross embodiment controller). Rather than overfitting to one reward function, Transformer Transformer is a dynamics model, whose reward-agnostic state and action predictions can be converted into reward-specific value predictions. These value predictions are used to steer embodiment diffusion towards high value robot designs, through a procedure we call Dynamics Self-Guidance. Experiments across multiple design spaces show zero-shot optimization of unseen rewards and trajectories, improving performance and runtime over the evolutionary baseline. Finally, we fabricated an optimized ALOHA design, which reduced tracking error by over 70% compared to the original design.

</details>


### [140] [Modular Robotic Catheters for Endovascular Aneurysm Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25807)
*Alex Ranne,Jinshi Zhao,Ali Anil Demircali,Songli Moey,Ayhan Aktas,Burak Temelkuran,Nassir Navab,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型2段式可操控导管系统及其驱动平台，可提升腔内手术的操作灵活性，减少手术时长与并发症。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前医师在腹主动脉腔内修复术中，使用传统器械较难顺利进入目标分支血管，导致手术时间延长和并发症增加，需要更灵巧的新型工具。

Method: 设计了具有4自由度的2段式可操控导管，并匹配可拓展、模块化的肌腱驱动平台。通过热熔纤维拉制技术和激光微加工制备导管本体，并在仿真和体外模型中测试其机械性能和操作性。

Result: 仿真评估了导管的弯曲刚度，体外实验证明该系统具有良好的可操控性，展示出在血管模型内的优越导航能力。

Conclusion: 该新型手持机器人导管可提升腔内血管手术效率，未来有助于解决复杂病例，缩短手术时间。

Abstract: Fenestrated/Branched endovascular aneurysm repair (FEVAR/BEVAR) require surgeons to navigate catheters and guidewires into various branches of the abdominal aorta, before deploying stent grafts to alleviate pressure on the aneurysm. Previous clinical studies suggests that surgeons continue to struggle with vessel access using standard commercial instruments, prolonging the procedural time and inducing further complications. In this work, we present two contributions to solving this problem: 1) A bespoke 2-segment steerable catheter, consisting of 4 degrees of freedom to enhance dexterity. 2) An expandable, modular tendon-driven actuation platform that can accommodate for the redundancies introduced in our system. To fabricate the catheter, we capitalized on thermal fiber drawing, a technique that creates high-aspect ratio devices at scale, and processed the catheter with laser micro-machining to soften its tip. We evaluated the system using simulations, where we investigated the catheter's bending stiffness, then its steerability with in-vitro experiments in vascular phantoms. This handheld, robotic steerable catheter system has the potential to shorten the length of future endovascular surgeries, and give clinicians the tools to resolve challenging clinical cases.

</details>


### [141] [HiFi-UMI: Learning Deployable Manipulation Policies from High-Fidelity UMI Data Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25895)
*Simple AI,:,Yuteng Wei,Jinming Ma,Jiawei Wang,Weitao Zhou,Yushen Zuo,Ke Rui,Minglei Li,Jinhao Zhang,Zhikang Pan,Xiang Wang,Haoran Jia,Huan Du,Zicheng Zeng,Jun Ma,Guiyu Qin,Di Zhang,Xiaofei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种高保真的非机器人控制（UMI）数据采集系统HiFi-UMI，实现了无需机器人后训练即可部署的操作策略，并开源了大规模高质量的数据集，极大提升了机器人学习的实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略的学习受限于高质量且可扩展数据的稀缺。真实机器人数据昂贵难以扩展，而传统高仿真UMI数据仅用于预训练，最终还是依赖小规模真实机器人数据做'锚定'。本研究试图突破这一限制，通过提高UMI数据的真实性，彻底移除对真实机器人“锚”的依赖。

Method: 设计并实现了便携的HiFi-UMI数据采集系统，专为高精度轨迹及手爪相对姿态的可靠获取而共研，采用离线头戴式双目惯导SLAM、原生相对姿态检测、微秒级同步GPIO触发、每手两枚超广角相机组成，无需外部追踪装置即可实现3毫米级末端精度。对比测试了三类政策模型在纯HiFi-UMI数据训练下的部署表现。

Result: 仅用HiFi-UMI演示数据后训练的策略，能直接在真实机器人上达到与原地遥操作接近甚至更优的效果。三种政策模型在典型任务成功率分别与基线相差-2.5、+3.1、-0.6个百分点，最优策略在精密插拔任务中成功率达85%；大规模预训练可使十项未见任务的动作误差降41%，单模型在任务成功率提升18.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 高保真的非机器人UMI数据足以替代传统小批量真实机器人数据'锚'，支持大规模、无需机器人参与的演示数据用于政策部署。作者公开了2,000小时微秒同步、宽视角高质量数据集，为机器人学习领域提供了重大发展基础。

Abstract: Learning deployable manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of data that is both high-fidelity and scalable. Real-robot teleoperation is accurate but costly to scale; robot-free UMI capture scales readily, and current practice uses the resulting data mainly for pre-training, adding a small real-robot "anchor" at post-training. We ask whether raising the fidelity of robot-free UMI data, rather than shrinking the real-robot fraction, can remove that anchor. We present HiFi-UMI, a portable UMI data-production system co-designed for trajectory accuracy, inter-gripper relative pose, synchronization, and field of view: head-mounted offline stereo-inertial SLAM, native rather than reconstructed relative pose, a shared microsecond GPIO trigger, and two wide-angle cameras per hand covering ~200 degrees. It reaches 3 mm workspace-local end-effector accuracy without external tracking infrastructure. Using this corpus, we demonstrate zero-robot post-training: a policy post-trained solely on HiFi-UMI demonstrations deploys directly on a real robot and matches in-domain teleoperation across three backbones spanning the vision-language-action and world-action-model families, with success-rate differences of -2.5, +3.1, and -0.6 percentage points on StarVLA-QwenPI, OpenPI-pi_0.5, and LingBot-VA; the strongest policy reaches 85% on a precision insertion task, even though the teleoperation baseline is collected in the evaluation scene and no HiFi-UMI trajectory is. Pre-training on 4,000 hours from the same corpus lowers action error on ten unseen tasks by 41% and, on StarVLA-QwenPI, raises real-robot success by a further 18.1 percentage points. We open-source HiFi-UMI-2K, 2,000 hours of microsecond-synchronized, ultra-wide-FoV demonstrations, each automatically reconstructed and validated through simulation replay, as a large-scale, high-fidelity resource for the robot-learning community.

</details>


### [142] [SAM3D-Guided Object-Centric Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25912)
*Zonghe Liu,Shanyuan Jie,Xiaoquan Sun,Chen Cao,Zetian Xu,Zongsheng Liu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对象的3D表示对齐框架，提升机器人在复杂场景下操作任务的精度和鲁棒性，无需在测试时额外的3D模块或深度信息支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型多依赖2D视觉，难以处理目标对象被遮挡、姿态变化、尺度变化等问题，限制了机器人在实际操作中的泛化能力和精细操作表现。

Method: 训练阶段，将SAM3D作为冻结的3D教师网络，从对象识别模型获得目标对象掩码，并提取密集的对象级3D表示。该表示与$π_0$中间视觉特征对齐，使策略网络在推理时内化3D对象信息，但在测试时依然采用原本只需RGB和语言输入的流程，无需3D或深度信息。

Result: 在仿真实验中，方法在LIBERO任务集取得99.1%表现，在CALVIN任务集获得4.11的平均长度。在真实世界场景中，方法在长时任务、多目标对象操作场景具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 引入3D对象表征并对其与视觉特征对齐，可极大提升基于视觉-语言的机器人操作泛化能力，且无需在推理时引入复杂的3D模块或传感器，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general robot manipulation, but most existing models rely on 2D visual-language backbones and lack fine-grained 3D understanding of target objects, especially under occlusion, pose variation, scale changes, and precise spatial interaction. We propose an object-centric 3D representation alignment framework built upon $π_0$, using SAM3D as a frozen 3D teacher to provide target-object 3D priors during training. Specifically, we localize task-relevant objects with object recognition models, generate corresponding object masks, and use SAM3D to extract dense object-level 3D representations, which are aligned with intermediate visual features of $π_0$. This enables the policy to internalize target-object 3D information while preserving the original RGB-language-to-action inference pipeline without requiring depth, point clouds, masks, SAM3D, or additional 3D modules at test time. Simulation experiments show consistent improvements, achieving 99.1\% on LIBERO and an average length of 4.11 on CALVIN. Real-world experiments further demonstrate that our method is particularly effective in long-horizon manipulation scenarios where the robot must focus on different target objects across multiple subtasks.

</details>


### [143] [DC-WAM: Dynamic-Centric Visual Supervision and Reasoning for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25918)
*Haoyuan Ji,Lingxiang Fan,Shang Su,Yinqiao Lu,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种全新动态中心的世界-动作模型（DC-WAM），通过优化视觉模态的使用方式，提升了机器人政策的泛化与控制能力，特别是在复杂环境干扰下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界-动作模型在预测未来视觉时多聚焦于逼真度，导致模型计算冗余且对控制任务无关特征（如纹理、光照、背景）分配了过多资源。研究动机是探究能否让模型更有效地学习与机器人控制相关的动态视觉特征，而不是外观为主的特征，从而提升模型鲁棒性与效率。

Method: 作者提出DC-WAM框架，对RGB视频分支的监督和推理流程进行动态中心调整。在监督环节，结合时序差分流匹配和轨迹引导权重，偏向于局部（如夹具、操控物体和接触区）的密集动态变化。在推理层面，引入DynaRoute预测每个token的动态相关性并加权注意力，引导模型关注与控制相关的未来变化。

Result: 在模拟与真实操作实验中，DC-WAM框架在各类环境扰动（光照、物体外观、背景纹理变化）下均显著提升了机器人策略的表现和泛化能力。

Conclusion: DC-WAM有效将视觉预测的关注点从外观特征转向与控制密切相关的动态变化，为高效、鲁棒的机器人感知与决策学习提供了新途径，特别适用于实际部署中多变环境场景。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies with future visual prediction, but it remains unclear what the visual modality should learn for control. While photorealistic future prediction provides dense supervision, it also incurs substantial computation and can allocate capacity to texture, illumination, and background variations that are only weakly related to action selection. Recent efficient WAM variants suggest that the main benefit of the video branch may not lie in the rendered future itself, but in the control-relevant visual representations induced during training. In this work, we revisit future video prediction from a dynamic-centric perspective and ask whether an existing RGB-based WAM can be redirected from appearance-dominated reconstruction toward interaction-induced visual dynamics without introducing additional modality-specific predictions or online inputs at deployment. We propose DC-WAM, a dynamic-centric WAM framework that redistributes supervision and computation in the RGB video branch. At the supervision level, DC-WAM combines temporal-difference flow matching with trajectory-guided weighting, emphasizing dense temporal changes and localized regions where the gripper, manipulated objects, and contact areas move. At the reasoning level, DynaRoute predicts token-wise dynamic relevance and converts it into an attention bias, guiding the model toward control-relevant future tokens. Experiments in simulation and on real-world manipulation tasks show that DC-WAM consistently improves policy performance, especially under out-of-distribution perturbations in lighting, object appearance, and background texture.

</details>
